**[00:26:39.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000357)  
Atty., may I interject?

**[00:26:41.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000358)

behind

vacant plantilia position?

**[00:26:51.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000363)

**[00:26:53.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000364)

**[00:26:55.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000365)

**[00:27:03.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000369)

**[00:27:11.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000373)

**[00:27:15.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000375)

**[00:27:17.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000376)

**[00:27:23.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000379)

**[00:27:25.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000380)

**[00:27:27.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000381)

**[00:27:29.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000382)

**[00:27:31.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000383)

**[00:27:33.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000384)  
positions. Marami po kami yung mga

**[00:27:35.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000385)  
contract service workers. Pero

**[00:27:37.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000386)  
ito, gaya po nito,

**[00:27:39.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000387)  
Department of Education, ano po yung number of vacancies?

**[00:27:41.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000388)

**[00:27:43.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000389)

Most of it would be teachers,

**[00:27:45.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000390)

**[00:27:45.900] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000390-split-2)  
Teachers.

**[00:27:46.500] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000391)  
57,000 something

**[00:27:49.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000392)

**[00:27:51.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000393)  
Yes, sir.

**[00:27:51.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000393-split-2)  
What is happening?

**[00:27:53.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000394)  
Kaya nga, I was asking you. Is it the budget?

**[00:27:55.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000395)  
What could be the reason?

**[00:27:56.650] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000395-split-2)  
Usually, if there

**[00:27:57.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000396)  
are plantilia positions already, sir, meron

**[00:27:59.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000397)  
yan naka-allocate na na-budget

**[00:28:01.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000398)

**[00:28:03.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000399)  
positions. But most of the

**[00:28:05.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000400)  
time, usually, it's finding

**[00:28:07.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000401)  
the fit sa qualifications

**[00:28:09.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000402)  
and the position being applied for.

**[00:28:11.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000403)  
So there may be a good number

**[00:28:13.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000404)  
of eligibles available out there to fill

**[00:28:15.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000405)  
up

**[00:28:17.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000406)  
vacant plantilia teacher positions.

**[00:28:19.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000407)  
However, yung mga available

**[00:28:21.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000408)  
na positions ay hindi pa

**[00:28:23.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000409)  
fit sa kanilang existing

**[00:28:25.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000410)  
qualifications kasi bawat grade

**[00:28:27.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000411)  
kasi or rank, may specific na

**[00:28:29.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000412)  
qualification standards that they should

**[00:28:31.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000413)  
meet. At the same time, I think what

**[00:28:33.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000414)  
complicates also the hiring process

**[00:28:35.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000415)  
in terms of plantilia positions, usually,

**[00:28:37.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000416)  
sir, mabagal

**[00:28:39.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000417)  
po ang hiring. May publication

**[00:28:41.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000418)  
and then you wait and then they go

**[00:28:43.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000419)  
through the human resource

**[00:28:45.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000420)  
recruitment

**[00:28:47.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000421)  
process. Then after the recruitment

**[00:28:49.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000422)  
process, may mga interview yan

**[00:28:51.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000423)  
and then ililista sila and then

**[00:28:53.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000424)  
dadaan sila sa last

**[00:28:55.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000425)  
leg na processing

**[00:28:57.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000426)  
before they can be recommended

**[00:28:59.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000427)  
for appointment at plantilia

**[00:29:01.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000428)  
position. On the other hand, for

**[00:29:03.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000429)  
COS, especially for matindi

**[00:29:05.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000430)  
na ang pangangailangan para sa servisyong

**[00:29:07.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000431)  
mga ganito, kasi karamihan ng

**[00:29:09.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000432)  
COS naman ay qualified sila

**[00:29:11.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000433)  
except for certain

**[00:29:13.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000434)  
types of requirements, qualified

**[00:29:15.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000435)  
sila for second level positions.

**[00:29:17.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000436)  
So it's easier to

**[00:29:19.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000437)  
recruit contract of service

**[00:29:21.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000438)  
workers.

po pwede

Usually, we put out

for anticipatory

but again, it's a discretion

**[00:30:06.200] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000461)  
of the agencies. Usually, advisory

**[00:30:08.020] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000462)  
lang po kami. Pero ang talaga

**[00:30:10.060] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000463)  
pong, palagay ko yun ang pinakamahirap

**[00:30:12.540] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000464)  
na

**[00:30:13.360] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000465)  
situation

**[00:30:15.720] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000466)  
being faced by our agencies.

**[00:30:17.880] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000467)  
Mabilis din po kasi ang pag-

**[00:30:19.700] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000468)  
alis na ngayon ng

**[00:30:21.540] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000469)  
empleyado sa gobyerno. I think

**[00:30:24.080] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000470)  
we are at a point, Your Honor,

**[00:30:26.060] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000471)  
na nag-generational shift.

**[00:30:28.060] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000472)  
Marami pong nag-retire,

**[00:30:29.220] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000473)  
especially today. I think this is observed by the heads

**[00:30:31.980] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000474)  
of agencies na marami ang retirees.

**[00:30:34.200] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000475)  
Hindi nakakahabol yung recruitment

**[00:30:35.680] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000476)  
kasi mabagal nga po yung proseso

**[00:30:37.880] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000477)  
ng recruitment.

**[00:30:38.400] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000477-split-2)  
What about promotions?

**[00:30:40.100] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000478)  
Promotions, same.

**[00:30:40.900] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000478-split-2)  
Of course. I mean,

**[00:30:42.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000479)  
pag kunwari example dito,

**[00:30:44.140] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000480)  
senior na ho ito, siguro

**[00:30:46.040] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000481)  
susunod, di po ba you have to

**[00:30:47.760] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000482)  
bring her or him up

**[00:30:49.880] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000483)  
para ma-fill up itong vacancy na ito.

**[00:30:52.420] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000484)  
And then, lahat naman po yan

**[00:30:53.980] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000485)  
mag-move. Kunwari teacher 1,

**[00:30:55.820] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000486)  
teacher 2, mapopromote si teacher 1 to teacher 2.

**[00:30:58.780] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000487)  
Yung mga mag-

**[00:30:59.200] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000488)  
nagaantay po dito, tataas po ito.

**[00:31:01.300] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000489)  
Who persuades

**[00:31:02.780] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000490)  
this agency's head of agency?

**[00:31:05.500] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000491)  
Can you do that? Can you ask them?

**[00:31:07.340] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000492)  
Like, Secretary,

**[00:31:09.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000493)  
kailan po kaya ma-fill up ito?

**[00:31:11.680] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000494)  
Or

**[00:31:12.060] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000495)  
administrator? Do you have the power

**[00:31:15.200] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000496)  
to remind them?

**[00:31:17.700] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000497)  
Attorney?

**[00:31:19.020] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000498)  
We can only remind

**[00:31:21.200] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000499)  
them, sir, but the final decision

**[00:31:23.080] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000500)  
rests with them.

**[00:31:25.420] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000501)  
Kasi like yung sinabi po ninyo na sana

**[00:31:27.080] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000502)  
dapat automatic succession na,

**[00:31:29.200] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000503)  
natitignan na lang. Hindi rin

**[00:31:31.000] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000504)  
pagbinsan, sir, kasi sometimes may nag-a-apply

**[00:31:33.160] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000505)  
ng mga external, outside of the agency

**[00:31:35.480] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000506)  
to occupy vacant

**[00:31:37.280] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000507)  
positions. So,

**[00:31:38.820] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000508)  
discretion ng agency kung papapasukin

**[00:31:41.460] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000509)  
sila or hindi. However,

**[00:31:43.220] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000510)  
at this point in time, we are studying a process

**[00:31:45.380] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000511)  
where para mas mapabilis po yung

**[00:31:47.260] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000512)  
pag-we know ng applicants

**[00:31:49.080] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000513)  
for a certain particular position,

**[00:31:51.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000514)  
i-focus na po sa competency.

**[00:31:53.680] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000515)  
Kasi ngayon, basta meron silang

**[00:31:55.180] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000516)  
four basic minimum qualifications like

**[00:31:57.360] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000517)  
educational achievement,

**[00:31:59.200] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000518)  
a number of training hours,

**[00:32:01.360] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000519)  
and then civil service eligibility

**[00:32:03.460] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000520)  
and then experience. Okay na yun.

**[00:32:05.900] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000521)  
Pero kunyari, sir, internal audit

**[00:32:07.700] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000522)  
yung ina-applyan niyang position, pero

**[00:32:09.420] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000523)  
lahat ng nag-a-apply, meron itong four basic

**[00:32:11.660] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000524)  
qualifications, pero walang auditor.

**[00:32:13.880] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000525)  
Pero ipapasok pa rin sila doon sa

**[00:32:15.500] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000526)  
proseso. Ang recommendation namin

**[00:32:17.540] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000527)  
ngayon is, pati itong apat,

**[00:32:19.800] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000528)  
kargahan na ng competency-based

**[00:32:21.440] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000529)  
na requirement para at the outset pa lang

**[00:32:23.580] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000530)  
kung wala siyang auditing

**[00:32:24.900] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000531)  
or background,

**[00:32:27.380] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000532)  
educational qualification or experience,

**[00:32:29.200] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000533)  
hindi na siya kasali sa

**[00:32:31.040] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000534)  
paligsahan para sa mga bakanteng

**[00:32:33.140] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000535)  
posisyon. It could

**[00:32:34.860] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000536)  
shorten, which we are trying

**[00:32:37.160] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000537)  
to implement now at the Civil Service Commission.

**[00:32:39.660] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000538)  
Mukhang napabilis po namin

**[00:32:41.000] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000539)  
ang some hirings for certain positions

**[00:32:42.960] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000540)  
in the Civil Service Commission.

**[00:32:45.000] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000541)  
Hopefully in 2027, we can make this

**[00:32:47.100] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000542)  
as an official policy of the Civil

**[00:32:49.140] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000543)  
Service Commission. Kasi ang shift po naman

**[00:32:50.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000544)  
natin ngayon is all competency-based,

**[00:32:53.760] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000545)  
hindi na lang po yung basta

**[00:32:54.960] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000546)  
minimum basic qualifications.

**[00:32:56.900] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000547)  
Thank you, Attorney. Before you proceed,

**[00:32:59.200] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000548)  
I'd like to acknowledge the presence

**[00:33:00.900] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000549)  
of our Secretary from Tourism,

**[00:33:03.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000550)  
Secretary Dita Angara Matay.

**[00:33:04.840] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000551)  
Thank you for joining us, ma'am. I know

**[00:33:06.460] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000552)  
why you're late. I was already informed because

**[00:33:08.760] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000553)  
you just came from that ASEAN thing

**[00:33:11.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000554)  
at PICC. But thank you for joining us.

**[00:33:13.520] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-000555)  
Magandang hapon po.

**[00:33:15.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000556)  
Continue, Attorney.

**[00:33:16.980] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000557)  
How many more minutes

**[00:33:18.680] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000558)  
is your presentation?

**[00:33:20.300] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000559)  
I think the budget na lang po.

**[00:33:22.660] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000560)  
Diretso sa budget na po.

**[00:33:24.300] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000561)  
Ang proposed budget namin are

**[00:33:26.200] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000562)  
4.04 billion. The next slide, please.

**[00:33:29.200] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000563)  
This just shows you

**[00:33:31.340] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000564)  
yung aming

**[00:33:33.680] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000565)  
budget request as against

**[00:33:35.480] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000566)

**[00:33:37.640] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000567)

Palagi pong mas maliit

**[00:33:41.680] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000569)

**[00:33:43.600] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000570)

**[00:33:45.700] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000571)

**[00:33:47.580] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000572)

And then the 2.56

For 2027, we requested

The NEP allows 4.04

**[00:34:11.300] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000584)

Assisted Project. And then the

**[00:34:23.040] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000590)  
2.58

**[00:35:17.680] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000617)  
And then maintenance

93.041

million kasi magdadagdag po

**[00:35:42.860] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000630)

And then yung PCSMP loan proceeds

ay dinagdagan po

merong increase ng

Yung mga

iba, capital

The next slide

Again,

personal services is at 1.882

**[00:36:27.480] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000652)  
billion or 46.65

**[00:36:48.920] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000662)  
The increases in M.O.O.E. and capital

**[00:36:51.040] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000663)  
outlay are for inflationary adjustments,

**[00:36:53.640] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000664)  
ICT requirements, and

**[00:36:54.900] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000665)  
PCSMP requirements.

**[00:36:57.360] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000666)  
The budget breakdown,

**[00:36:59.200] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000667)  
according to cost

**[00:37:00.060] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000668)  
structure,

**[00:37:02.400] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000669)  
ito po yung comparison ng

**[00:37:05.180] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000670)  
2026 at saka 2027.

**[00:37:08.000] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000671)  
The most significant increase

**[00:37:09.620] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000672)  
is really in foreign assisted

**[00:37:11.220] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000673)  
projects. From 334 million

**[00:37:13.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000674)  
in 2026 to 1.453

**[00:37:16.140] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000675)  
billion in

**[00:37:17.140] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000676)  
2027. Operations and

**[00:37:19.380] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000677)  
general administration and support

**[00:37:20.980] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000678)  
also increased pero very

**[00:37:23.240] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000679)  
minimal while support operations is

**[00:37:25.260] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000680)  
lower in 2027 simply

**[00:37:27.280] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000681)  
because most of the program

**[00:37:29.200] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000682)  
the PCSMP program will

**[00:37:31.560] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000683)  
now support certain

**[00:37:35.340] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000684)  
operations in program implementation.

**[00:37:39.960] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000685)  
Next slide, please. Ito na yung mas

**[00:37:42.060] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000686)  
detailed na breakdown ng cost structure.

**[00:37:45.900] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000687)  
4.04 billion shows foreign

**[00:37:49.360] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000688)  
assisted projects account for the largest

**[00:37:51.440] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000689)  
share or 35.98 percent

**[00:37:54.540] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000690)  
supporting our civil service modernization

**[00:37:56.700] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000691)  
initiatives. General administration and support

**[00:37:59.060] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000692)  
accounts for 26.44 percent while operations

**[00:38:03.500] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000693)  
receives 24.61 percent. The remaining

**[00:38:07.340] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000694)  
allocations are support to operations at

**[00:38:09.400] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000695)  
332 million or 8.21 percent. Retirement at life

**[00:38:14.440] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000696)  
insurance premiums which is automatic

**[00:38:17.120] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000697)  
appropriations at 149 million which is

**[00:38:20.700] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000698)  
3.68 percent and locally funded projects at

**[00:38:23.900] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000699)  
44 million. Minimal increase of 1.08 percent simply because of

**[00:38:29.060] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000703)  
inflationary adjustments. Ito lang po

**[00:38:32.240] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000704)  
yung susunod na slide summary ng new and automatic

**[00:38:38.220] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000705)  
appropriations. From 2026 up to 2027. From 2024 to 2026 the

**[00:38:51.340] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000706)  
CSC appropriation had a minimal increase. Only in 2026

**[00:38:55.760] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000707)  
where the appropriation was 2.898,

**[00:38:59.060] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000708)  
of which was P334 billion to PCSMP.

**[00:39:04.100] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000709)  
The significant increase in the 2027 proposed budget is largely attributable to the foreign-assisted project,

**[00:39:11.180] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000710)  
amounting to P1.453 billion or 35.98% of the total budget.

**[00:39:17.620] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000711)  
So, if you look at all the figures, this budget is dedicated actually to doing the PCSMP

**[00:39:25.560] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000712)  
because it is supposed to modernize the Philippine civil service system.

**[00:39:30.100] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000713)  
We have also the historical utilization of the Commission's appropriations from 2022 to 2027.

**[00:39:36.480] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000714)  
From 2022 to 2025, the Commission achieved 100% in obligation and disbursement rates.

**[00:39:44.280] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000715)  
For 2026, as of June, the CSC had PHP 285 billion and allotments of this amount,

**[00:39:53.260] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000716)  
P1.142 billion has been obligated.

**[00:39:55.560] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000717)  
Equivalent to 39.46% or this is the budget utilization rate.

**[00:40:02.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000718)  
And P1.143 billion has been disbursed, equivalent to 39.39%, which is the disbursement rate.

**[00:40:11.460] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000719)  
In sum, the NEP for 2026 was 4.18.

**[00:40:18.360] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000720)  
Para lang comparative, we were given 2.89.

**[00:40:21.860] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000721)  
Ngayon, hinihingi po natin 4.04 billion.

**[00:40:25.560] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000722)  
Just to reiterate, 1.45 is for the PCSMP and regular subsidy is 2.587 billion.

**[00:40:34.960] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000723)  
The increase is primarily intended, again, to support our modernization priorities.

**[00:40:41.820] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000724)  
For 2027 targets, we will just skim through them.

**[00:40:45.900] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000725)  
But our 2027 targets will be supporting our three priority program pillars,

**[00:40:54.440] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000726)  
human

**[00:40:55.560] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000727)  
resource governance,

**[00:40:56.820] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000728)  
professionalization,

**[00:40:58.780] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000729)  
and workplace cooperation

**[00:41:00.920] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000730)  
and

**[00:41:01.800] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000731)  
adjudication,

**[00:41:05.900] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000732)  
administrative adjudication.

**[00:41:09.140] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000733)  
So human resource governance, we will advance the prime ESG transition.

**[00:41:12.960] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000734)  
We will be not discontinuing, slowly transitioning from prime HRM to prime embedded systems governance.

**[00:41:21.860] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000735)  
Kasi magta-transform na ito sa digital systems.

**[00:41:25.560] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000736)  
Babaguhin ang pag-i-implement ng four pillars of the human resource from recruitment, selection, and placement, performance, management,

**[00:41:40.820] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000737)  
learning and development, and recognition, and rewards for the civil service.

**[00:41:48.820] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000738)  
At i-embed dito yung tinatawag natin, human resource management information system,

**[00:41:53.820] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000739)  
umang Hurman system, and payroll system,

**[00:41:56.280] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000740)  
which hopefully will digitalize all these processes and um transactions,

**[00:42:01.820] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000741)  
Huia human resources will be monitored in real time.

**[00:42:04.640] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000742)  
Mas mabilis pang pagpasok ng datos.

**[00:42:06.820] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000743)  
Mas mabilis ang evaluation assessment kung umaandar talaga ang human resource practices in our human resource offices.

**[00:42:15.200] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000744)  
Kasi sa prime HRM.

**[00:42:16.720] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000745)  
All documents based.

**[00:42:18.640] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000746)  
Kung may panahon na may mag-check or may mag-assess, doon lang titignan.

**[00:42:23.120] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000747)  
E yung naka-folder at nakalagay sa dokumento, baka last six months ago pa ginawa,

**[00:42:28.280] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000748)  
ngayon iba na yung datos at hindi siya up-to-date.

**[00:42:31.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000749)  
Medyo document-based, hindi siya accurate measure of how we manage our human resources in government.

**[00:42:38.200] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000750)  
It will take a long time because the pilot for the PCSMP is 40 departments, 40 agencies.

**[00:42:46.740] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000751)  
But in case na merong mga agencies na hindi po makakapapasok sa transition sa Prime ESG,

**[00:42:56.560] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000752)  
nag-gunguha po kami ng dagdag na 20 agencies to fill in if there are people who will not be able to get into the transition

**[00:43:06.680] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000753)  
so that yung target ng PCSMP na at least 40 agencies of government for its first rollout phase will be net.

**[00:43:16.740] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000754)  
We will be transitioning, we will finish the transition period for Prime ESG

**[00:43:23.460] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000755)  
and then we will initially go live and testing for the system, HRMIS-PS.

**[00:43:29.300] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000756)  
We will also be fully implementing the CS data management system.

**[00:43:36.460] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000757)  
Wala po kasi tayo ngayon mabilis, accessible at reliable na database ng lahat ng personal data.

**[00:43:46.740] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000758)  
Or information ng lahat ng ating mga empleyado sa gobyerno.

**[00:43:50.260] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000759)  
Let me just ask, bakit ngayon lang po ito?

**[00:43:55.380] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000760)  
I mean, how long have you been requesting the government to modernize yung ating CSC?

**[00:44:03.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000761)  
3 million po itong empleyado natin dapat po.

**[00:44:06.900] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000762)  
At saka this is government, ikaw nga, we're talking of government institutions, personnel.

**[00:44:13.040] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000763)  
Bakit ngayon lang po ito?

**[00:44:14.660] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000764)  
How long have you requested?

**[00:44:16.740] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000765)  
To modernize CSC?

**[00:44:20.820] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000766)  
Pumasok lang po ako sa Civil Service sa buntot po ng 2024.

**[00:44:26.600] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000767)  
Have you already requested it since 2024?

**[00:44:30.540] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000768)  
Yes, yes.

**[00:44:30.720] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000769)  
What happened?

**[00:44:31.900] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000770)  
Ito nga po, itong paglabas po.

**[00:44:34.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000771)  
Ngayon pa lang?

**[00:44:35.700] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000772)  
Ang nirequest po namin noong 2025, pero ganun din po.

**[00:44:38.540] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000773)  
In-approve po yung loan, yung TOR ng loan ng second quarter ng 2025.

**[00:44:46.740] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000774)  
Mayroon pong proseso na pag-aaralan ng Department of Justice.

**[00:44:50.200] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000775)  
Are you saying, attorney, may mga files tayo na nakalagay pa sa folder?

**[00:44:55.760] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000776)  
Karamihan po.

**[00:44:56.980] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000777)  
Tao natin?

**[00:44:57.840] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000778)  
Karamihan po.

**[00:44:58.740] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000779)  
So haanapin?

**[00:45:00.000] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000796)  
Kung nari hahanapin si Alfredo Flores, saan ba yung letter A, anong agency siya?

**[00:45:07.180] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000797)  
Opo, nakakalungkot man pong tanggapin pero karamihan ng ating records sa civil service ay paper-based.

**[00:45:12.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000798)  
Have you asked yung mga matagal na po dyan sa CSC anong nangyari?

**[00:45:16.840] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000799)  
Because we heard that kayo po yung repository ng ikang employment na nasa gobyerno tapos naka-folder pa.

**[00:45:25.340] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000800)  
Kaya po urgent po yun?

**[00:45:26.160] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000801)  
Hanggang ngayon?

**[00:45:26.720] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000802)  
Opo, marami po kasi limited lang po.

**[00:45:29.440] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000803)  
Some have digitized, in fairness.

**[00:45:31.900] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000804)  
Digitization started somewhere in 2022 and 2023.

**[00:45:37.200] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000805)  
Pero ang migration po kasi, like scanning lang po, limited din po ang scanners namin.

**[00:45:42.440] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000806)  
Matagal na hindi po nag-request if you can see the history of the budget.

**[00:45:45.920] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000807)  
I've been hearing, Attorney Chairman Yap, about this modernization.

**[00:45:51.660] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000808)  
Since time immemorial pa ito, wala pa nga itong administration na ito.

**[00:45:56.840] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000809)  
They're talking about ikang modernization.

**[00:45:59.440] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000810)  
In time of Duterte, atras pa tayo, time of Pinoy, they've been talking about modernizing our system.

**[00:46:06.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000811)  
Bakit ho, anong nangyari dito?

**[00:46:08.060] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000812)  
I mean, I cannot understand.

**[00:46:09.300] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000813)  
Well, I cannot blame you, but have you asked people na nariyan na sa CSC bago ka pa dumating na kung anong nangyari?

**[00:46:18.600] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000814)  
Haven't they requested?

**[00:46:20.180] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000815)  
I think, sir, it's more of a mindset sa ating mga tao sa pamahalaan.

**[00:46:25.440] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000816)  
Ang tingin po kasi nila sa modernization, sa digitization.

**[00:46:29.440] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000817)  
Basta may laptop na, may computer na, parang nandun na tayo.

**[00:46:36.440] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000818)  
Pero hindi nila nakikita yung, they have to be used as a unified system to process documents.

**[00:46:43.440] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000819)  
Hindi po nakarating sa ganong pag-isip.

**[00:46:46.440] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000820)  
Napabilis ang pag-type, napabilis ang pag...

**[00:46:48.440] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000821)  
Malay natin itong sinasabi kong itong isang tao dito, nagtatrabaho sa isang department, nagsasideline sa isang department.

**[00:46:56.440] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000822)  
How would you know?

**[00:46:57.440] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000823)  
Exactly.

**[00:46:57.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000824)  
Kung wala sa system.

**[00:46:58.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000825)  
Whereas kung ipapunch mo yung pangalan niya...

**[00:47:00.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000826)  
Magalaman po ka.

**[00:47:01.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000827)  
For example, Erwin Tulba, dito ka pala nagtatrabaho sa Senado, tapos meron ka pang sideline sa department na ganito.

**[00:47:06.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000828)  
Opo.

**[00:47:07.440] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000829)  
Kaya kung put out mo yung record, iba yung record na doon, iba yung record.

**[00:47:10.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000830)  
Tama po. Tama po kayo.

**[00:47:11.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000831)  
Hindi naguusap.

**[00:47:12.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000832)  
Do you think may mga ganyan tayo na nagmu-moonlight na mga employees because hindi pa po modernize tayo?

**[00:47:19.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000833)  
What is your assessment?

**[00:47:20.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000834)  
I cannot discount...

**[00:47:21.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000835)  
So let me ask your opinion.

**[00:47:22.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000836)  
I cannot discount the possibility na meron pong ganon.

**[00:47:25.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000837)  
Kasi hindi naman po mabilis maghukay ng paper.

**[00:47:27.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000838)  
Exactly.

**[00:47:28.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000839)  
Paper documents.

**[00:47:29.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000840)  
Kaya nga po ang una naming step is meron na po tayong electronic na po ang PDS, personal data sheet.

**[00:47:36.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000841)  
So ang submission is electronic, diretso na po sa database, ikaklassify na po natin.

**[00:47:41.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000842)  
At mas madali pong ma-access for policy planning and program development.

**[00:47:46.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000843)  
Kasi pag mag-planning ka ng succession, alam mo kung gaano katagal ang... ilan ang average years in government.

**[00:47:52.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000844)  
Ano yung mga retirement periods.

**[00:47:54.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000845)  
Right.

**[00:47:55.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000846)  
Gaano ka katagal mag-i-estate.

**[00:47:57.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000847)  
Kaya is that also the reason why matagal makakuha ng pensyon yung mga ibang employees natin dahil kailangan hanapin pa.

**[00:48:05.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000848)  
Sabi nandoon pumunta ka sa letter H.

**[00:48:08.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000849)  
Opo.

**[00:48:09.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000850)  
Tapos pa puro alikabuk na. May agyo pa nga.

**[00:48:12.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000851)  
Opo. Mas masakit pa po pag inulan yan at natunaw.

**[00:48:15.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000852)  
Ay.

**[00:48:16.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000853)  
Pinanay.

**[00:48:18.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000854)  
That's very sad. Pero anong sinasabi na since time immemorial walang plano. Panahon nila Pinoy.

**[00:48:26.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000855)  
I mean, let's backtrack.

**[00:48:28.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000856)  
Naririnig ko na po yung usapan na yan. I don't know what's going on. And it's a very serious problem.

**[00:48:35.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000857)  
I mean, yung mga staff ko, diba, kailangan na, dapat nandyan sila sa repository po ninyo.

**[00:48:42.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000858)  
Opo.

**[00:48:43.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000859)  
And if you're a repository, eh dapat hindi nyo po mga folder-folder lang paano pag kinain ng daga.

**[00:48:49.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000860)  
Kaya nga po urgent yung aming mga programs kasi ano.

**[00:48:53.300] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000861)  
Okay. Well, I support you on this. Good. Continue po. How many more minutes do you have there?

**[00:48:57.920] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000865)  
Kontina lang po siguro.

**[00:48:59.000] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000866)  
Go ahead, because we still have a Career Executive Service Board and of course our DOT.

**[00:49:04.180] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000867)  
Opo. Yung mga iba na lang po ay, like yung Learning... Everything kasi po pwedeng digitalized from Learning and Management Systems.

**[00:49:11.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000868)  
And then pati yung, fantasia po kasi namin na pag ang registration para sa exam ng Civil Service ay continuing registration

**[00:49:20.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000869)  
para hindi nagkaklag yung system.

**[00:49:23.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000870)  
Dereder. Basta register ka lang ng registration.

**[00:49:24.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000871)  
I-register ka lang ng register tapos tatawagan ka and then ikaw ay number 500 para ka sa August. Meron na lang cut-off, continuing na po yung registration. Tapos digitize na rin pati yung pag-exam and within one day, yun ang aming balak for 2027, makuha mo yung results kasama na yung kopya ng iyong eligibility.

**[00:49:43.820] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000872)  
Kasi ngayon po ang eligibility namin ay nasa CSEVs na authenticated na hindi ka na kailangang sumulat at magpaano. The one that you will download from the CSEVs of the Civil Service will now be considered as an authenticated eligibility document.

**[00:49:58.800] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000873)  
So kasama po ito sa mga gagawin namin. And then we hope also to digitize our legal, our adjudication system para mabawasan po, mas mabilis po ang processing ng kaso at mawala na po yung mga aging cases na dumarating hanggang tatlo.

**[00:50:13.640] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000874)  
So apat na taon. Kasi patay na yung humihingi ng ayuda or ng legal assistance, eh hindi pa natatapos yung kanyang kaso.

**[00:50:22.660] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000875)  
Overall, these initiatives for the 2027 are designed to create more integrated digital and efficient systems that will improve how the CSEV delivers services to the Civil Service.

**[00:50:33.920] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000876)  
Sana po, bigyan niyo po ako ng pagkataon na i-present yung aming mga...

**[00:50:39.200] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000877)  
Baka naman po, maawa po kayo sa aming...

**[00:50:43.640] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000878)  
Nagdagan po ninyo ang budget namin kasi hindi naman po kami nakakapaghingi pa except dito sa PCSMP ng malaking budget.

**[00:50:51.900] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000879)  
Kasi maraming na po sa aming regional infrastructure by priority.

**[00:50:56.020] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000880)  
Regional offices and field offices, nakikituloy lang po kami sa mga local government units or other offices in the regions.

**[00:51:04.440] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000881)  
At karamihan sa kanila ay either kulang na po ng space for us.

**[00:51:09.960] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000882)  
Yung isa po namin sa...

**[00:51:13.640] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000883)  
My next question, para mapadali tayo, Chairman.

**[00:51:17.860] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000884)  
Yes po.

**[00:51:18.880] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000885)

**[00:51:27.140] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000886)  
I think...

**[00:51:28.640] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000887)  
Nasa letter po namin...

**[00:51:31.740] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000888)  
Yeah, 783 million.

**[00:51:32.800] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000889)  
Yes po.

**[00:51:34.160] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000890)  
Nakalista po doon.

**[00:51:35.020] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000891)

**[00:51:40.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000892)  
At saka, kagaya po sinasabi, sana mo pagbigyan kayo.

**[00:51:43.640] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000893)

**[00:51:48.500] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000894)  
Ano po ang effect nito sa services ng CSE?

**[00:51:52.060] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000895)  
Well, in the meantime, we will make do. Pero ang importante po kasi yung mga regional field offices at saka major field offices.

**[00:52:00.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000896)  
Nilista po namin sa submission namin na doon sa buo, may we request that we submit it na lang, sir.

**[00:52:06.720] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000897)  
Kagaya po nitong sa Region 3, sir, sa San Fernando.

**[00:52:09.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000898)  
Well, anyway, ma'am,

**[00:52:11.620] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000899)  
Sasagasaan po ng national highway.

**[00:52:13.200] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000900)  
Your budget will be brought before the plenary and then masasama po ito sa BICAM.

**[00:52:17.120] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000901)  
Yung details po, ibibigay po namin.

**[00:52:18.760] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000902)  
Please submit it to the committee, ma'am.

**[00:52:20.620] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000903)  
Yung mga iba po is operational mobility kasi seven years old na po ang mga fleet namin.

**[00:52:25.560] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000904)  
Karamihan na po, mas mahal po i-repair at i-maintain kesa yung bumili na lang po ng bago, especially for regional operations po.

**[00:52:33.120] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000905)  
At yung huli po, mayroon po kami sanang hingin kasi dahil mataas ang power requirements ng itong modernization program,

**[00:52:41.620] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000906)  
we would like to establish a solar energy project at the central office para yung frontline services hindi po na-upend ng mga brownouts at saka pag may bagyo and then...

**[00:52:55.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000907)  
How much would that be?

**[00:52:56.600] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000908)  
Ang initial po na mura lang naman sa initial po kasi 22 million po.

**[00:53:01.980] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000909)  
Pwede ho nating ilaban yan. Alright.

**[00:53:05.620] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000910)  
Yun lang po. Maraming salamat po sa inyong panahon.

**[00:53:08.500] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000911)  
Thank you.

**[00:53:08.960] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000912)  
I just have a... Yeah, yun yung...

**[00:53:11.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000913)  
My last question for you.

**[00:53:13.100] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000914)  
Okay.

**[00:53:16.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000915)  
What do you do dun sa mga empleyado natin na sa gobyerno na walang kapit, na walang koneksyon?

**[00:53:22.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000916)  
Marami po ako naririnig na ganito.

**[00:53:25.840] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000917)  
Tagal ko na po, senator, 15 years na po.

**[00:53:28.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000918)  
Hindi po ako ma-promote kasi wala po akong kapit, wala po akong connect.

**[00:53:33.400] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000919)  
Sabi ko, nag-accomplish mo na ba? May mga study ka na ba? Masteral?

**[00:53:37.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000920)  
Mayroon na po eh.

**[00:53:38.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000921)  
Ito na nga po, doctorate na po ako eh.

**[00:53:40.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000922)  
Pero hindi pa po ako na po-promote. Bakit?

**[00:53:43.720] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000923)  
Wala pong connection eh. Yung pinili po, yung junior sa akin, na wala namang qualification.

**[00:53:48.820] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000924)  
What do you do when you hear this?

**[00:53:51.260] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000925)  
May naririnig ba kayong ganito in your office as a chairman?

**[00:53:55.460] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000926)  
Marami po. Marami po.

**[00:53:57.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000927)  
What do you do?

**[00:54:01.680] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000928)  
Osa, can you please tell the other room to keep it down?

**[00:54:07.340] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000929)  
Kung sino man yung mga bisita nila dyan.

**[00:54:10.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000930)  
Go ahead, chairman.

**[00:54:12.660] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000931)  
Yung mga ganun po. Again, sir, parang hands-tied kami kasi discretion po ito ng agency head kasi appointing authority sila.

**[00:54:22.340] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000932)  
Pero pag dumating naman po sa amin, kung yung qualifications niya talaga ay kulang, kasi i-check po namin eh.

**[00:54:30.760] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000933)  
Pero ibig sabihin, nangyayari pa rin ba ito at this day and age na kompleto na po siya ng requirement?

**[00:54:38.840] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000934)  
Yan po ang problema sa gobyerno natin.

**[00:54:40.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000935)  
Hindi po na po promote because walang connect.

**[00:54:43.780] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000936)  
Graduate na po siya lahat na po ng requirement.

**[00:54:46.340] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000937)  
Na-accomplish niya na hanggang sa magre-retire na lang siya.

**[00:54:49.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000938)  
Hindi po siya ma-promote-promote kasi wala po siyang connect.

**[00:54:52.600] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000939)  
Or kung kasi, hindi sa ma-promote siya, nilalagyan dito, nilalagyan doon kung saan saan galing yung mga tao na para sa pwesto niya.

**[00:55:01.500] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000940)  
So what do you do about it?

**[00:55:04.080] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000941)  
Kaya po namin, well, it will not be a solution to the problem.

**[00:55:08.540] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000942)  
But we are going to certify na po yung mga...

**[00:55:10.620] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000943)  
HRMO, yung mga magpaproseso po ng mga ano.

**[00:55:14.080] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000944)  
And we are now, we are doing an overhaul of qualification standards.

**[00:55:20.060] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000945)  
Meron ho tayong tinatawag na para mag-shift na siya sa competency base.

**[00:55:24.880] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000946)  
Don't you ask your regional offices and then to check on government agencies to see who is due for promotion,

**[00:55:35.140] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000947)  
who needs to be promoted sa agencies na ito?

**[00:55:38.020] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000948)  
Or ask their HRMO?

**[00:55:40.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000949)  
HR offices na submit, sino ba ang for promotion?

**[00:55:47.980] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000950)  
I think this is the problem, Chairman.

**[00:55:51.760] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000951)  
Kaya nakita ko na may mga vacancies po tayo, di ba?

**[00:55:55.680] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000952)  
Na hindi po na-fill up kasi wala na po-promote dahil nga walang kapit or something.

**[00:56:02.120] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000953)  
Iba ang nailalagay.

**[00:56:04.140] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000954)  
Ito talaga ang problema sa gobyerno natin.

**[00:56:06.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000955)  
And most of us here.

**[00:56:09.320] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000956)  
Most of us here.

**[00:56:10.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000957)  
I'm sure na-experience one time or another.

**[00:56:13.980] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000958)  
Nag-gusto niyo ma-promote, kabatang-bata siya, lawyer siya, doktor siya, o ano man siya dyan,

**[00:56:19.420] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000959)  
sa City Health, o Municipal Health, sa Department of Justice, nagsisigasig.

**[00:56:25.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000960)  
Pero hindi po ma-promote kasi wala pong connect eh.

**[00:56:27.880] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000961)  
Mauna itong si Atty. Tagpulano rito, nakaibigan ni Secretary o ni Director, yun po yun eh.

**[00:56:34.600] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000962)  
Real talk lang po tayo.

**[00:56:36.300] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000963)  
How do we solve this issue now?

**[00:56:38.700] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000964)  
Kasi may naririnig pa rin po.

**[00:56:40.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000965)  
Hanggang ngayon na gano'n pa rin po ang problema.

**[00:56:43.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000966)  
I think that should be your mission, ma'am.

**[00:56:45.900] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000967)  
I think yung HRMISPS, sir, kasi naka-actual na na-upload mo yung mga records ng mga tao.

**[00:56:53.720] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000968)  
So this will solve that problem?

**[00:56:55.820] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000969)  
It will, I think.

**[00:56:56.700] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000970)  
Kasi makikita na po namin eh.

**[00:56:57.980] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000971)  
Ngayon hindi po kami makapasok sa ENSA para tignan yung actual records nila.

**[00:57:02.960] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000972)  
Totoo bang ito ang bakante sa inyo?

**[00:57:04.700] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000973)  
Pwede po nilang i-hide sa amin.

**[00:57:07.660] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000974)  
Dito, actual time, they have to submit it already.

**[00:57:10.620] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000975)  
So yung yun, makikita mo, ah, hindi nyo pinifill up ito, bakit?

**[00:57:14.960] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000976)  
Ah, ito pala, marami nang pwedeng i-promote.

**[00:57:17.260] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000977)  
Bakit hindi kayo nag-promote?

**[00:57:18.960] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000978)  
O matagal na pala, bakit hindi kayo nagpa-publish ng vacant positions?

**[00:57:23.020] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000979)  
Tapos yung kanilang HRMPSB, yung Promotions Board, Selection Board,

**[00:57:27.000] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000980)  
pati yung papeles nila, isasubmit dito sa system eh.

**[00:57:30.040] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000981)  
So makikita na ngayon ng system na, ah, itong ahensya na ito, kulang dito, kulang dito, kulang dito.

**[00:57:35.480] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000982)  
Then we can do the remedial measures with data, evidence,

**[00:57:40.620] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000983)  
based na po yung action po namin.

**[00:57:42.400] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000984)  
Sila mag-file ng complaint sa CSC.

**[00:57:44.780] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000985)

**[00:57:47.320] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000986)

**[00:57:49.740] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000987)  
Ako po ay, ah, 25 years na po ako rito sa gobyerno.

**[00:57:54.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000988)  
Dalaga pa po ako, may apo na po ako, hindi ako ma-promote, promote.

**[00:57:58.060] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000989)  
Ah, ah, parang awa mo na.

**[00:58:00.880] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000990)

**[00:58:02.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000991)  
Kasi may mga dumadam po sa office ko.

**[00:58:04.120] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000992)  
Opo, opo.

**[00:58:04.840] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000993)  
Na maging congressman ako, senador, nagpapapromote.

**[00:58:07.720] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000994)  
Sabi ko, ayaw ko na, tingnan ko nga.

**[00:58:09.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000995)  
Kompleto naman.

**[00:58:10.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000996)  
So qualification, hindi lang talaga.

**[00:58:12.600] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000997)  
Pag kinausap mo yung director, regional director,

**[00:58:14.980] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000998)  
o kung ano man yan dyan, manager,

**[00:58:16.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000999)  
kung ano-ano sinasabi sa akin,

**[00:58:18.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001000)  
kaya hindi pa qualified, kaya time in grade, time in position.

**[00:58:22.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001001)  
Pero qualified naman, ho.

**[00:58:23.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001002)  
So, I really do not understand.

**[00:58:25.400] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001003)  
Pwede ba sila magreklamo sa inyo na,

**[00:58:27.900] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001004)  
ikang, ah, na hindi siya napopromote?

**[00:58:32.060] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001005)  
Walang, wala kasing specific na kaso sa hindi napopromote.

**[00:58:36.800] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001006)  
Unless, unless...

**[00:58:38.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001007)  
Wala po kayong, ah, ikang department.

**[00:58:39.860] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001008)

**[00:58:40.620] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001009)  
But, meron ho kaming public assistance sa program.

**[00:58:44.840] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001010)  
Bigay po nila sa amin yung complaint.

**[00:58:46.800] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001011)  
Maganda po ang record namin doon sa contact center ng bayan,

**[00:58:49.800] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001012)

**[00:58:51.300] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001013)

**[00:58:55.580] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001014)

**[00:58:57.220] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001015)

**[00:58:58.820] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001016)

**[00:59:10.620] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001018)

**[01:00:00.000] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001048)  
Thank you. Good afternoon, our distinguished budget sponsor, Honorable Senator Erwin Tulfo, to Chairperson of Civil Service Marilyn Barua Yap, Chairperson Urduja Alvarado.

**[01:00:14.980] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001049)  
Ladies and gentlemen, good afternoon. I am pleased to present the proposed budget of the Career Executive Service Board for fiscal year 2027.

**[01:00:24.100] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001050)  
Our proposed budget will support the CES Board in delivering and fulfilling its unique mandate enshrined in Presidential Decree No. 1 to promulgate rules, standard procedures in the selection, classification, compensation, and career development of the members of the CES.

**[01:00:42.240] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001051)  
It is noteworthy that our mandate covers the four pillars of the human resource management in the third level.

**[01:00:51.300] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001052)  
Our budget is aligned with the Philippine Development Plan 2023 to 2028.

**[01:00:56.800] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001053)  
Under Chapter 14, Outcome 4, where competent, motivated, resilient public servants are supported, it specifically provides that the CES system will be strengthened through merit and fitness, where the potential and qualified third-level officials will undergo the four-stage CES eligibility examination process, the CES leadership management proficiency program, and other lifelong learning programs for appointment to CES

**[01:01:24.100] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001054)  
rank to build up a critical mass of CES officers and eligibles. It complements our mission to infuse the bureaucracy with well-selected and development-oriented leaders, [unclear] bring change, expertise and leadership for responsive public service. Under our PREXC, the CESB operations is focused on the core program of career executive screening and development to ensure and promote merit and fitness for entry to the CES and sustaining a continuing pool of CES

**[01:01:54.100] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001055)  
eligibles and CESOs. Our proposed budget for 2027 in summary, originally we proposed 219 million but we were only provided with 142.857 million under the National Expenditure Program. Our 2027 budget per NEP consists only of personnel services

**[01:02:24.100] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001056)  
and MOOE without capital outlay. This NEP, our budget in the NEP, represents an increase of 34% from our previous 2026 budget due to the 58% increase in MOOE and 8% increase in personnel services. The 58% increase in MOOE for 2027 is mainly because of the issuance of the Permanent Committee Resolution 2026-01 issued in July 2026.

**[01:02:58.300] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001057)  
The Permanent Committee composed of the Secretary of the Budget and Management, Department of Finance Secretary and COA Chairperson, requiring the closure of the CESB revolving fund beginning 2027. The Permanent Committee granted limited authority to the CES Board to utilize fees collected from our examination and training activities through a revolving fund

**[01:03:24.100] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001058)  
until the end of 2026 only. Thus, we are required to close the CESB revolving fund account. Hence, the CESB was advised to include all its funding requirements for the 2027 budget under the GAA or the General Appropriations Act. The proposed increase in the MOOE will cover 31 million, 31.6 million in the NEP to cover the MOOE for the core program operations which were previously charged

**[01:03:54.100] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001059)  
to the revolving fund. It consists of eligibility examination process, appointment and promotion in CES rank, CES capacity building, CES performance management, recognition and executive placement. Consequently, in view of the Permanent Committee resolution, we are also proposing the inclusion of the phrase examinations, assessment, performance validation and panel interviews in Section 13 of the general appropriations,

**[01:04:24.100] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001060)  
general provisions of the 2027 GAA, as these items are not in the current GAA provision stipulations, to enable us to utilize the budget in the NEP for the implementation of our core programs. On top of the NEP, we are requesting for reconsideration of the Senate Committee on Finance for the approval of additional budget request of 66.917, 66.9 million.

**[01:04:54.100] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001061)  
This includes prioritized budget items under the original proposal amounting to 18.9 million consisting of re-fleeting of six motor vehicles classified as unserviceable and for replacement under the DBM circular, 2022-1.

**[01:05:11.500] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001061-split-2)  
Executive Director Ballesteros, hindi po nila sinama ba yan yung request ninyo ng transportation na yan?

**[01:05:17.000] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001061-split-3)

**[01:05:17.350] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001061-split-4)

**[01:05:18.200] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001061-split-5)

**[01:05:25.000] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001062)

**[01:05:25.600] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001062-split-2)  
13 million, 251,000.

**[01:05:31.660] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001062-split-3)  
Okay, I'll help you out. Go ahead.

**[01:05:33.380] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001062-split-4)  
Okay. So also some equipment for the approved new plantilla positions in our Eligibility and Rank Appointment Division, and also for the regular repairs and maintenance of our building and some professional services for management and monitoring inspection of the construction of the new CESB

**[01:05:54.100] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001063)  
building at UP Diliman under the budget of the DPWH. And we also request, Honorable Senator, the additional funding requirement for the total rehabilitation, rehabilitation of the electrical system and related structural repairs in our old CESB building.

**[01:06:14.160] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001063-split-2)  
Kasama na po ba diyan sa 66.9?

**[01:06:17.500] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001063-split-3)  
Yes po. Yes po.

**[01:06:18.500] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001063-split-4)  
Okay.

**[01:06:19.000] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001063-split-5)  
Sa ngayon po kasi hindi namin ma-occupy ang aming old building and we are renting a temporary space.

**[01:06:24.900] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001064)  
Sa House of Representatives, pinaprubahan po nila ito?

**[01:06:27.350] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001064-split-2)  
Yes po.

**[01:06:28.000] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001064-split-3)  
Alright.

**[01:06:28.600] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001064-split-4)  
Thank you so much po.

**[01:06:30.700] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001064-split-5)  
Okay.

**[01:06:31.650] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001064-split-6)  
So we have already requested the technical assistance of the Quezon City Government Engineering Department for the comprehensive rehabilitation plan of our electrical system and the repair works. This was not included in our original budget proposal as the emergency blackout incident happened after the deadline of submission of our budget to the DBM.

**[01:06:54.100] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001065)  
So these are just some slides. So, Honorable Sponsor, Senator Tulfo, we are therefore respectfully requesting the Senate Finance Committee's approval of our total proposed budget of 209.774 million consisting of the NEP and the additional 66.917 million for mission-critical priority budget items for

**[01:07:24.100] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001066)  
reconsideration of the Honorable Finance Committee. This will finance the priority programs of the CESB for FY 2027 consisting of career executive screening and development, namely eligibility examination process, appointment, promotion to CES rank, CES capacity building, professional development, performance management, recognition, executive placement and external relations of our career

**[01:07:54.100] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001067)  
executives; the formulation, implementation, monitoring of policies, regulations, ruling, legal opinions on the concerns of our CES officials; and general administration, support to operations including information systems development. Next slide. So this program will finance the four-stage CES eligibility process. We will, with this budget, we will be

**[01:08:24.100] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001068)  
able to serve our nationwide clients. We have a total of applicants for eligibility of 5,742, where 3,702 has already passed the CES written exam. We have also processed the assessment of 2,278 AC applicants and they are now moving towards the panel validation and panel interview for conferment of CES eligibility. We hope also to be

**[01:08:54.100] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001069)  
proactive in reaching out to the 1,319 incumbents of CES positions to assist them in their CES eligibility process. To show the size of the CES community we are serving, as of June 2026 we have in our roster 3,569 total active CESOs and CES eligibles. CESB caters to their needs as they participate in our lifelong learning programs, performance evaluation system conducted semestrally and executive placement on demand.

**[01:09:24.100] CESB Executive Director Maria Marcy Cosare-Ballesteros** (asr-001070)

**[01:16:00.700] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001088)  
As remarked, September 28th as the World Tourism Day, Philippine tourism moves forward with renewed momentum.

**[01:16:10.700] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001089)  
The latest World Economic Forum Index ranks the Philippines 7th among the world's fastest improving tourism economies.

**[01:16:19.920] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001090)  
Our task is to sustain this progress and translate it into greater opportunities for our people.

**[01:16:26.200] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001091)  
With this, may we present to you the Department of Tourism.

**[01:16:30.000] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001092)  
The Department of Tourism's proposed Fiscal Year 2027 Budget.

**[01:16:37.180] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001093)  
Tourism is delivering measurable economic value, employment, and regional opportunity.

**[01:16:43.920] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001094)  
Sustained and strategic investment is necessary to protect and expand these gains.

**[01:16:49.000] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001095)  
Today, we will cover

**[01:16:51.700] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001096)  
First, Tourism Sector Performance

**[01:16:54.260] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001097)  
Second, DOT's Fiscal Year 2027 National Expenditure Programme

**[01:17:00.000] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001098)  
Third, Priority Funding Requirements

**[01:17:03.580] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001099)  
Under the Tourism Act of 2009, the DOT leads the planning, development, regulation, and promotion of Philippine tourism.

**[01:17:14.740] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001100)  
Together with our attached agencies, DOT covers the entire value chain for the tourism industry,

**[01:17:21.100] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001101)  
from destination and infrastructure development, heritage and parks management, dive safety and retirement tourism,

**[01:17:28.760] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001102)  
to domestic and

**[01:17:29.980] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001103)  
international market promotion.

**[01:17:31.980] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001104)  
Tourism performance is measured through four indicators.

**[01:17:35.980] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001105)  
Direct economic value, tourism expenditure, employment, and arrivals.

**[01:17:40.980] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001106)  
In 2025, tourism generated 2.27 trillion in tourism direct gross value added, equivalent to 8.1% of GDP.

**[01:17:51.980] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001107)  
Domestic tourism expenditure reached 3.26 trillion.

**[01:17:56.720] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001108)  
Inbound tourism expenditure reached 698 billion.

**[01:18:01.720] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001112)  
Total internal tourism expenditure reached 3.96 trillion.

**[01:18:06.720] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001113)  
Tourism also supported 7.70 million jobs in 2025, or 15.7% of total employment.

**[01:18:15.720] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001114)  
This exceeds the pre-pandemic level of 6.49 million jobs.

**[01:18:20.720] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001115)  
Today, about one in every six Filipino workers is employed in tourism-related activities.

**[01:18:27.640] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001116)  
International arrivals continue to recover. In the first semester of 2026, arrivals reached 3.41 million, up from 3.31 million in the same period of 2025. Within ASEAN, the Philippines improved from seventh to sixth place in tourism arrivals from 2024 to 2025, and we maintain the fifth place in tourism receipts,

**[01:18:53.980] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001142)  
generating

**[01:18:55.960] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001143)  
approximately 12.15

**[01:18:58.180] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001144)  
billion in 2025.

**[01:19:00.280] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001145)  
These results were achieved

**[01:19:01.740] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001146)  
despite a significant resource

**[01:19:03.560] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001147)  
gap. Among comparable

**[01:19:05.560] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001148)  
ASEAN countries with available data,

**[01:19:08.360] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001149)  
the Philippines had the lowest

**[01:19:09.760] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001150)  
tourism budget in 2025

**[01:19:11.860] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001151)  
at approximately

**[01:19:13.280] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001152)  
49.72 million.

**[01:19:15.820] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001153)  
The implication is clear.

**[01:19:17.940] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001154)  
Philippine tourism is producing

**[01:19:19.300] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001155)  
stronger returns despite limited

**[01:19:21.620] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001156)  
resources, with focus

**[01:19:23.520] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001157)  
investments, the sector can deliver

**[01:19:25.640] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001158)  
more jobs, more visitor spending,

**[01:19:28.200] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001159)  
and more opportunities for

**[01:19:29.580] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001160)  
local communities.

**[01:19:31.500] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001161)  
The department is translating

**[01:19:33.580] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001162)  
its resources to measurable

**[01:19:35.180] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001163)  
interventions. For workforce

**[01:19:37.400] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001164)  
development, DOT trained

38,867

**[01:19:42.860] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001166)  
tourism stakeholders

**[01:19:43.880] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001167)  
in the first semester of

**[01:19:45.300] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001168)  
2026. Training is now

**[01:19:47.640] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001169)  
focused on quality, industry

**[01:19:49.820] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001170)

For standards, the

**[01:21:43.040] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001227)  
Index. As a

**[01:21:49.020] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001230)  
May papanood ba sila?

**[01:21:53.500] Presentation video** (asr-001231)  
Have you ever received a gift

**[01:21:57.540] Presentation video** (asr-001232)  
you thought was

**[01:21:59.080] Presentation video** (asr-001233)  
already amazing,

**[01:22:01.200] Presentation video** (asr-001234)  
only to discover there was

**[01:22:03.500] Presentation video** (asr-001235)  
even more waiting inside?

**[01:22:06.380] Presentation video** (asr-001236)  
That's the Philippines.

**[01:22:08.340] Presentation video** (asr-001237)  
Familiar in name,

**[01:22:09.840] Presentation video** (asr-001238)  
but endless in discovery.

**[01:22:13.140] Presentation video** (asr-001239)  
Beyond the familiar,

**[01:22:14.860] Presentation video** (asr-001240)  
unfolding

**[01:22:15.520] Presentation video** (asr-001241)  
layer by layer,

**[01:22:18.220] Presentation video** (asr-001242)  
are hidden codes,

**[01:22:20.100] Presentation video** (asr-001243)  
unforgettable flavors,

**[01:22:21.380] Presentation video** (asr-001244)  
and stories that reveal

**[01:22:23.320] Presentation video** (asr-001245)  
themselves, one discovery

**[01:22:25.980] Presentation video** (asr-001246)  
at a time.

**[01:22:27.500] Presentation video** (asr-001247)  
Tourism partners across the

**[01:22:29.260] Presentation video** (asr-001248)  
country have come together to make

**[01:22:31.300] Presentation video** (asr-001249)  
every journey easier,

**[01:22:33.800] Presentation video** (asr-001250)  
more affordable,

**[01:22:35.300] Presentation video** (asr-001251)  
and more rewarding than ever

**[01:22:37.200] Presentation video** (asr-001252)  
so that every visit

**[01:22:38.960] Presentation video** (asr-001253)  
reveals something new.

**[01:22:41.240] Presentation video** (asr-001254)  
And every new discovery feels

**[01:22:43.160] Presentation video** (asr-001255)  
a little like first love.

**[01:22:45.700] Presentation video** (asr-001256)  
Unexpected

**[01:22:46.580] Presentation video** (asr-001257)  
and impossible to forget.

**[01:22:52.020] Presentation video** (asr-001258)  
Introducing a

**[01:22:53.200] Presentation video** (asr-001259)  
new tourism platform.

**[01:22:55.200] Presentation video** (asr-001260)  
Your gateway to discover,

**[01:22:57.200] Presentation video** (asr-001261)  
plan,

**[01:22:59.200] Presentation video** (asr-001262)  
and book your journey.

And together,

who knows?

We all might just find

**[01:23:09.200] Presentation video** (asr-001266)  
the one.

Discover more to love.

Where every journey brings you closer

to the one.

**[01:23:21.200] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001270)  
Under the Love Philippines brand, the

**[01:23:23.200] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001271)

**[01:23:25.200] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001272)  
is designed to convert promotion into actual bookings and spending.

**[01:23:31.200] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001273)  
In its first 100 days, the campaign generated more than 203,000 airline seats sold,

**[01:23:37.200] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001274)  
over 800 room nights, and 1.79 million in documented hotel sales.

and disbursement

**[01:24:05.200] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001283)

**[01:34:55.040] DOT Undersecretary Ma. Dionesa A. Rivera-Guillermo** (asr-001528)

**[01:38:55.560] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001604)

**[01:38:57.640] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001605)

**[01:39:03.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001607)

**[01:39:08.900] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001609)

**[01:39:13.600] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001612)  
Sa trip.

**[01:39:14.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001613)

**[01:39:17.320] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001614)

**[01:39:30.100] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-001617)

**[01:42:14.720] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001679)

**[01:42:20.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001682)  
With regard po sa outbound

**[01:42:22.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001683)

**[01:42:24.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001684)  
Last year,

**[01:42:26.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001685)  
nasa 7.6 million po

**[01:42:28.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001686)

**[01:42:30.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001687)  
Pero ito ay mas maliit naman po

**[01:42:32.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001688)

**[01:42:34.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001689)
