**[01:42:36.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001690)  
naman, based on our

**[01:42:38.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001691)  
household survey on

**[01:42:40.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001692)  
domestic visitors, nasa

**[01:42:42.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001693)  
140 million po

**[01:42:44.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001694)  
ang nagbiyahe within the

**[01:42:46.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001695)  
Philippines. So when it comes

**[01:42:48.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001696)  
naman po sa expenditure,

**[01:42:50.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001697)  
if you take a look at the

**[01:42:52.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001698)  
expenditure ng mga Pilipino

**[01:42:54.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001699)  
going abroad, this is about

**[01:42:56.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001700)  
357 million

**[01:42:58.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001701)  
pesos only, compared

**[01:43:00.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001702)  
to the expenditures that

**[01:43:02.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001703)  
they do here, which is around

**[01:43:04.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001704)  
3.26 trillion po.

**[01:43:06.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001705)  
So, karamihan

**[01:43:08.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001706)  
pa rin ang Pilipino ay

**[01:43:10.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001707)  
bumabiyahe sa loob ng Pilipinas

**[01:43:12.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001708)  
at malaki rin ang kanilang naidudulot na

**[01:43:14.180] DOT Undersecretary Verna Esmeralda C. Buensuceso** (asr-001709)  
economic impact po.

**[01:43:16.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001710)  
Pero yung ticket po kasi, ma'am,

**[01:43:18.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001711)  
sabi lang, magbibiyahe ako

**[01:43:20.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001712)  
d'yan, mas mahal. Kaming

**[01:43:22.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001713)  
12,000, 15,000

**[01:43:24.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001714)  
sa Hong Kong, may discount pa

**[01:43:26.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001715)  
sa Cebu Pacific na piso fare.

**[01:43:28.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001716)  
Makakaalis siya sa halagang 4,000

**[01:43:30.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001717)  
piso. Yan ho yung problema

**[01:43:32.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001718)  
natin, di po ba? Tapos number

**[01:43:34.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001719)  
two, imbis na

**[01:43:36.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001720)  
malapit lang po, punta

**[01:43:38.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001721)  
pa siya ng Clark, sasakay

**[01:43:40.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001722)  
pa siya ng bus, tapos

**[01:43:42.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001723)  
pag-landing na doon siya ulit, e

**[01:43:44.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001724)  
taga-Para?aque siya. Paano ho yan?

Can everybody answer?

**[01:43:48.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001726)  
Yusek Stanley?

**[01:43:50.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001727)  
Your Honor, thank you.

**[01:43:52.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001728)  
So, alam ho natin at may

**[01:43:54.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001729)

**[01:43:56.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001730)  
Yun po yung

**[01:43:58.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001731)  
reason bakit tumataas po ang

**[01:44:00.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001732)  
airfare. Meron ho tayong

**[01:44:02.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001733)  
demand, mataas po ang demand, which is a good

**[01:44:04.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001734)  
thing for us. Maraming gustong mamasyal

**[01:44:06.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001735)  
po dun sa mga lugar na iyon.

**[01:44:08.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001736)  
However, ang problema natin

**[01:44:10.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001737)  
ay yung mga infrastructure natin,

**[01:44:12.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001738)  
yung runway po. Sa mga

**[01:44:14.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001739)  
provinces, may iksi

**[01:44:16.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001740)  
ang runway. So, limited ang

**[01:44:18.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001741)  
capacity na papasok doon.

**[01:44:20.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001742)  
So, tulad ng isang

**[01:44:22.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001743)  
turbopropeller na aeroplano pong lumilipad

**[01:44:24.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001744)  
doon, kaya lang ho mga

**[01:44:26.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001745)  
80 na passengers. So,

**[01:44:28.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001746)  
hindi pa siya night-rated ho. So,

**[01:44:30.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001747)  
limited pa rin ang operating

**[01:44:32.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001748)  
hours po doon. So, nagiging

**[01:44:34.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001749)  
very expensive kasi mataas

**[01:44:36.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001750)  
ho yung demand. Eh, yung

**[01:44:38.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001751)  
number of seats going there is

**[01:44:40.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001752)  
maliit. Tapos,

**[01:44:42.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001753)  
pwede na rin natin ihahambing ho sa

**[01:44:44.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001754)  
Manila Airport, no?

**[01:44:46.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001755)  
Compare sa other airports

**[01:44:48.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001756)  
like na ma-mention nyo ho, yun sa

**[01:44:50.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001757)  
mga Vietnam, Thailand,

**[01:44:52.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001758)  
parallel ho yung runway nila. So,

**[01:44:54.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001759)  
tayo dito, nag-accommodate tayo

**[01:44:56.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001760)  
mga 40 movements per hour yung

**[01:44:58.180] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001761)  
airport natin. Pag for a single

**[01:45:00.080] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001762)  
runway ang pinaka-efficient na yung

**[01:45:01.900] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001763)  
Gatwick Airport. Naka-50 plus

**[01:45:03.980] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001764)  
movement sila. However,

**[01:45:05.900] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001765)  
pag yun parallel ang operation ng runway,

**[01:45:08.400] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001766)  
para tuloy-tuloy yung runway

**[01:45:09.900] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001767)  
isa, pang take-off lang, yun isa

**[01:45:11.920] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001768)  
landing. So, mabilis ho ang movement.

**[01:45:14.260] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001769)  
Easily na do-doble ho yung capacity.

**[01:45:16.720] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001770)  
So, kung tumaas ho

**[01:45:18.040] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001771)  
yung capacity, nagkakaroon

**[01:45:20.040] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001772)  
tuloy ng ano, parang nakakapag

**[01:45:22.320] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001773)  
yung economies of scale po,

**[01:45:25.060] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001774)  
tsaka dumadami ho.

**[01:45:26.340] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001775)  
Pati yung hotel po na-mention

**[01:45:28.060] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001776)  
na rin, kulang rin tayo ng

**[01:45:29.880] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001777)  
hotel rooms po. Kaya po yung

**[01:45:32.260] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001778)  
kukompare natin

**[01:45:34.160] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001779)  
sa ibang bansa ho,

**[01:45:36.620] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001780)  
medyo mas mahal talaga

**[01:45:38.060] DOT Undersecretary Stanley K. Ng** (asr-001781)  
yung pag-travel sa atin.

**[01:47:06.900] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001825)

**[01:47:12.620] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001828)

**[01:47:15.100] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001829)  
We are the Tourism

**[01:47:16.480] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001830)

**[01:47:21.700] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001831)

**[01:47:22.540] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001832)  
mandate is centered on three key areas.

**[01:47:24.840] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001833)

**[01:47:26.760] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001834)

**[01:47:28.880] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001835)

**[01:47:30.920] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001836)

**[01:47:34.060] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001837)  
tourism and industry capability through

**[01:47:36.360] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001838)  
targeted marketing and stakeholder

**[01:47:38.380] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001839)  
development

**[01:47:39.620] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001840)  
and third, positioning MICE destinations for

**[01:47:42.300] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001841)

**[01:47:44.960] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001842)  
exhibitions. So together,

**[01:47:47.040] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001843)  
these efforts help drive visitor

**[01:47:48.740] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001844)  
growth, industry covetativeness and

**[01:47:50.900] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001845)  
sustainable tourism development for our

**[01:47:52.760] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001846)  
country. Next slide, please.

**[01:47:55.160] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001847)  
So, our DBM confirmed NEP level

**[01:47:58.480] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001848)  
is 1.1 billion.

**[01:48:00.360] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001849)  
We originally proposed 1.3 billion,

**[01:48:03.540] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001850)

**[01:48:07.180] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001851)

**[01:48:10.000] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001852)  
Next slide.

**[01:48:12.900] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001853)  
60.7% of our 2027 COB

**[01:48:16.880] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001854)  
goes directly to marketing and promotions.

**[01:48:19.080] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001855)  
We are keeping all 160 authorized and filled positions.

**[01:48:23.920] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001856)  
And we've retained 67.9 million

**[01:48:26.500] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001857)

**[01:48:28.380] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001858)  
to absorb emerging priorities during the year.

**[01:48:32.020] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001859)  
Next slide.

**[01:48:36.260] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001860)

**[01:48:41.320] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001861)  
So, this year, our international arrivals

**[01:48:44.280] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001862)  
across our 13 key markets rose

**[01:48:47.420] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001863)  
from 2.46 million to 2.6 million.

**[01:48:52.060] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001864)  
And our programs,

**[01:48:53.560] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001865)  
we were able to implement more programs this year.

**[01:48:56.500] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001866)  
Versus last year.

**[01:48:57.860] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001867)  
From 26 last year, ngayon, 46 na.

**[01:49:01.280] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001868)  
Sales leads generated also grew, no?

**[01:49:04.340] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001869)  
This year versus last year.

**[01:49:06.360] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001870)  
So, last year, yung sales leads natin

**[01:49:08.140] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001871)  
were 2.79 billion.

**[01:49:10.480] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001872)  
This year, it's already at 5.51 billion.

**[01:49:14.340] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001873)  
For the business-to-business meetings

**[01:49:16.280] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001874)  
that we were also able to set up,

**[01:49:19.580] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001875)

**[01:49:21.440] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001876)

**[01:49:25.680] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001877)  
Next slide.

**[01:49:26.500] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001878)  
So, for actual sales,

**[01:49:30.640] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001879)  
this ties back to the sales leads figures

**[01:49:32.980] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001880)

**[01:49:34.820] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001881)

**[01:49:39.180] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001882)  
So, we were able now to gather actual sales

**[01:49:42.160] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001883)

**[01:49:45.400] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001884)  
So, as you can see,

**[01:49:46.640] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001885)  
2024 had actual sales of 152 billion,

**[01:49:52.040] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001886)

**[01:49:56.500] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001887)  
Next slide.

**[01:50:00.120] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001888)

**[01:50:03.860] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001889)

**[01:50:05.580] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001890)

**[01:50:10.160] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001891)

**[01:50:14.720] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001892)

**[01:50:19.180] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001893)

**[01:50:22.800] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001894)  
Next slide.

**[01:50:26.500] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001896)

**[01:50:27.720] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001897)

To summarize, FY2027,

**[01:52:23.200] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001936)  
our proposed COB at the Tourism Promotions Board

**[01:52:25.940] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001938)

**[01:52:32.860] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001940)

**[01:52:37.860] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001942)

**[01:52:39.720] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001943)  
Thank you, COO Nograles.

**[01:52:43.100] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001944)  
Quick question lang, ma'am.

**[01:52:45.340] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001945)  
You're requesting for 1.1 billion.

**[01:52:48.100] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001946)  
Tama po ba yun?

**[01:52:49.060] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001947)  
Actually, Mr. Chair,

**[01:52:51.680] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001948)  
we requested for 1.3 billion from the DBM

**[01:52:54.300] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001949)  
and they give us 1.1 billion

**[01:52:57.520] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001950)  
from our SAG-F.

**[01:53:00.100] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001951)  
So they are the ones that give us the budget ceiling

**[01:53:04.020] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001952)  
that we can use in a year.

**[01:53:05.740] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001953)  
All right.

**[01:53:06.320] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001954)  
See, this is what I was telling Mr. Chair,

**[01:53:09.200] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001955)  
our ComSec,

**[01:53:10.420] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001956)  
that I need a DBM here

**[01:53:11.800] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001957)  
because my next question is about DBM.

**[01:53:14.360] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001958)  
Anyway, yung TPB po,

**[01:53:16.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001959)  
Tourism Promotions Board,

**[01:53:17.780] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001960)  
has 25.7 billion

**[01:53:20.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001961)  
unallocated balance

**[01:53:22.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001962)  
sitting in our National Treasury.

**[01:53:24.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001963)  
What specific steps,

**[01:53:26.380] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001964)  
have you taken

**[01:53:27.120] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001965)  
to seek DBM's assistance

**[01:53:29.140] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001966)  
to gain access to these funds?

**[01:53:32.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001967)  
Why are they remaining idle?

**[01:53:34.020] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001968)  
Sayang naman po.

**[01:53:35.400] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001969)  
Marami po kayong paggagamitan.

**[01:53:37.040] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001970)  
Kailangan po natin i-promote

**[01:53:38.360] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001971)  
na i-promote ang ating turismo.

**[01:53:40.360] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001972)  
Ano po nangyayari dito?

**[01:53:41.400] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001973)  
Have you checked on this?

**[01:53:42.600] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001974)  
Have you asked DBM

**[01:53:43.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001975)  
who's always absent?

**[01:53:46.620] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001976)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[01:53:47.840] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001977)  
Actually,

**[01:53:49.280] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001978)  
so let me read quickly.

**[01:53:50.600] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001979)  
So the TPB SAG-F,

**[01:53:52.220] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001980)  
yung hung fund na

**[01:53:53.800] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001981)  
that we just mentioned,

**[01:53:54.800] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001982)  
is accessed through

**[01:53:55.980] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001983)  
the regular government budgeting process.

**[01:53:59.560] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001984)  
The availability of funds

**[01:54:00.940] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001985)  
in the SAG-F

**[01:54:01.780] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001986)  
does not by itself

**[01:54:03.160] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001987)  
authorize TPB to spend the funds.

**[01:54:05.940] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001988)  
TPB proposes its programs

**[01:54:07.900] TPB COO Margarita Montemayor Nograles** (asr-001989)  
and funding requirements

**[01:59:58.180] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002181)  
But...

**[02:00:00.000] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002182)  
Kung makikita niyo po, sir, yung arrivals natin is mataas po versus Singapore, for example, and Thailand.

**[02:00:09.940] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002183)  
So for every 1 million US dollars that we invest in marketing, we get 74,758 tourists or travelers.

**[02:00:18.740] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002184)  
Ngayon po, yung Vietnam, if you will see, sir, it's an anomaly, sir.

**[02:00:22.000] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002185)  
Because it's the private sector po talaga yung nagdadrive ng kanilang tourism in terms of investments and promotions.

**[02:00:32.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002186)  
So what's the proposal of the agency now for the budget for branding?

**[02:00:41.760] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002187)  
So for 2027, sir, we are proposing 1 billion for branding and promotions alone.

**[02:00:48.880] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002188)  
And I can give you the breakdown, sir.

**[02:00:52.000] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002189)  
Yeah, so I mentioned earlier that we are in the middle of bidding out our media agencies.

**[02:00:57.780] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002190)  
So next year, once this is awarded, we will bid out another media agency to extend or to supplement the current efforts of the Refresh Love the Philippines campaign.

**[02:01:10.260] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002191)  
We are allocating 200 million pesos for that, followed by partnerships and co-branding activations.

**[02:01:17.440] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002192)  
Ito po yung partnership natin with the private sector.

**[02:01:20.300] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002193)  
So for example, yung club companies.

**[02:01:22.000] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002194)  
So co-branding with PAL or from other markets, local markets po, such as, for example, sa Korea, where we are doing interventions, yung mga local tour operators po nila.

**[02:01:32.820] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002195)  
And then we are also allocating 25 million for campaign monitoring, analytics, reporting, and social listening.

**[02:01:39.520] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002196)  
So right now, sir, ito po yung budget that we are allocating so that we get the social sentiment online or even, and parang be informed.

**[02:01:52.000] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002197)  
So if what, be informed of the campaigns that we are putting out there and whether they're working or not.

**[02:02:00.440] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002198)  
And then we're also allocating 25 million for brand health tracker.

**[02:02:04.440] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002199)  
So this is naman po what will diagnose basically the Refresh Love the Philippines campaign.

**[02:02:11.480] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002200)  
So what we'll do is we are creating like a brand metrics agency such as Kantar Nielsen.

**[02:02:22.000] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002201)  
So we're bidding it out.

**[02:02:23.580] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002202)  
We will do an analysis or diagnosis of, we will have them diagnose our, the pre-launch of the Refresh Love the Philippines.

**[02:02:34.980] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002203)  
So this is the social, the current state that we are in.

**[02:02:37.680] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002204)  
And then once we launch, ano po natin, the reception of the markets, target markets abroad, key markets and emerging markets.

**[02:02:46.040] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002205)  
And then six months after, ano po yung naging resulta nito?

**[02:02:48.980] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002206)  
Tumaas ba ang public sentiment towards the Philippines?

**[02:02:52.000] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002207)  
So yun po yung sa brand health tracker.

**[02:02:54.540] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002208)  
And then we are allocating 100 million for sub-brand and global media campaign.

**[02:03:00.320] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002209)  
So ito naman po yung, for, para po ito yung discover more to love natin.

**[02:03:06.600] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002210)  
So we are not replacing Love the Philippines, but rather we are coming up with like mga maliliit na sub-campaigns po.

**[02:03:11.880] DOT Assistant Secretary Armand Lorenze V. Sapitan** (asr-002211)  
So we are.

**[02:03:12.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002212)  
I have one quick question, if you would, si Chair, kay Secretary dito siguro.

**[02:03:18.640] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002213)  
Parang tayo lang ang bansa na paiba-iba.

**[02:03:21.300] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002214)  
Bawat administration.

**[02:03:22.000] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002216)  
May love, may more love, may love-love, may more fun, may wow, may wow Philippines, kung ano-ano.

**[02:03:30.460] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002217)  
Every time na may bagong administration, bakit ganun?

**[02:03:33.560] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002218)  
Wala tayong tatakman eh. Unlike Malaysia, truly Asia.

**[02:03:38.220] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002219)  
Ay mga ganun eh, diba?

**[02:03:39.820] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002220)  
Kaya mamaya-maya, pustahan tayo. Pag pumalitan ng administration, hindi na love yan.

**[02:03:44.220] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002221)  
Hate ba yan? Kung ano man yan.

**[02:03:46.160] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002222)  
Come to the Philippines, enjoy the Philippines.

**[02:03:49.140] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002223)  
Why do we change?

**[02:03:50.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002224)  
Parang I don't see anything wrong with wow Philippines, talagang dumami ho, tapos pinalitan, tapos papalitan, it's more fun ngayon, love, love the Philippines, baka next administration na ito, come to the Philippines, mamuhay ka sa Philippines, what is going on in here?

**[02:04:10.180] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002225)  
Yes sir, I totally agree with you because we should not have really kept on changing the tagline po.

**[02:04:17.040] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002226)  
So ang una ko pong ginawa, nung dumating,

**[02:04:20.540] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002227)  
Ako, love the Philippines, I will preserve love the Philippines po.

**[02:04:25.740] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002228)  
So kung makikita niyo po lahat ng campaign namin, it's love the Philippines.

**[02:04:29.720] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002229)  
May konting, depending po anong campaign, anong season o anong target market ang inaanon namin, nagkaroon ng discover more to love.

**[02:04:39.180] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002230)  
But that still emanates from the organic love the Philippines.

**[02:04:43.440] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002231)  
Bakit po discover more to love?

**[02:04:44.940] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002232)  
Kasi po, nung dumating kami, ako po,

**[02:04:47.920] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002233)  
yung ano, there's a very sharp drop in that sentiment for travel, plus mahal pa tayo.

**[02:04:55.480] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002234)  
So sabi ko, instead of our tourism assets becoming stranded, let's launch a domestic tourism campaign.

**[02:05:02.880] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002235)  
And that's what we actually did.

**[02:05:04.600] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002236)  
So pag love the Philippines, sa kababayan po natin, baka nakatira dito,

**[02:05:09.840] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002237)  
discover more to love because we are highlighting the hidden gems.

**[02:05:13.400] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002238)  
Hindi lang yung nakita nyo, hindi lang yung alam nyo, marami po pa yan.

**[02:05:17.300] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002239)  
So it's still under the love the Philippines.

**[02:05:19.820] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002240)  
At hindi nilang po discover Philippines. Parang ganun.

**[02:05:22.260] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002241)  
Or my next question, Secretary, do we, must we pass a law para hindi po bago-baguhin nila ito?

**[02:05:30.000] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002242)  
Parang pinaglalaruan.

**[02:05:31.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002243)  
Pag ako nung naisip ng bagong appoint na Secretary, palitan ko kaya ito dahil hindi ko gusto.

**[02:05:39.020] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002244)  
Yung nakaraang Secretary may credit nito.

**[02:05:41.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002245)  
So sabihin, yung susunod na DOT Secretary, itong love ni Secretary Mata, hindi ko gusto ito eh.

**[02:05:47.300] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002246)  
Siya ang naalala ng mga tao, dapat meron akong sarili.

**[02:05:50.960] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002247)  
Every administration, meron tayong bago.

**[02:05:54.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002248)  
Why don't we stick? Kailangan ba ng batas?

**[02:05:56.680] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002249)  
Kasi kung kailangan ma'am, then we'll have to file a law na huwag nyo na pakialaman niyang branding natin.

**[02:06:02.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002250)  
Kasi paiba-iba eh.

**[02:06:04.420] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002251)  
Right on the bat sir, kung pwede po, i-legislate na natin yun.

**[02:06:08.620] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002252)  
Kasi anyway, maganda naman yung love the Philippines.

**[02:06:11.040] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002253)  
Kasi ang ating asset po sir, is the people are the best asset.

**[02:06:16.100] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002254)  
And there's a lot of things.

**[02:06:17.400] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002255)  
From our caregivers overseas, from our seaman too.

**[02:06:20.640] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002256)  
And then we have more here.

**[02:06:21.780] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002257)  
Kasi ang hospitality ng Pilipino po, at saka ang lambing, hindi mo papantayan sa ASEAN o kahit sa ibang lugar.

**[02:06:28.940] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002258)  
So if we can legislate and we use love the Philippines, I think I'm all for it sir.

**[02:06:35.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002259)  
I agree with the concern of our chair.

**[02:06:38.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002260)  
I think we should really stick with one branding.

**[02:06:43.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002261)  
Kasi pagpaiba-iba, yun ang problema sa Pilipinas eh.

**[02:06:47.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002262)  
Ano yung pag may change of administration, pinapalitan din lahat.

**[02:06:51.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002263)  
Kaya nga, binpushing ni sir chair yung comprehensive, yung master plan for infrastructure and national development.

**[02:06:58.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002264)  
Pero dapat kasama na rin ito sa tourism.

**[02:07:00.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002265)  
At least we will have our, yung recall kasi it's difficult to achieve.

**[02:07:05.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002266)  
It takes years or decades.

**[02:07:09.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002267)  
Kaya nga yung Malaysia through Asia, talagang yun lang.

**[02:07:12.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002268)  
Nagsistake.

**[02:07:12.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002269)  
So I'm hoping that we will this time, we will stick.

**[02:07:17.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002270)  
To it, no?

**[02:07:18.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002271)  
Kasi siguro from, it's more from the Philippines, tapos nag-love the Philippines, rebranding tayo.

**[02:07:25.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002272)  
That takes a lot of effort and expense also.

**[02:07:29.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002273)  
Dapat nga nag-double tayo kasi rebranding yun eh, no?

**[02:07:33.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002274)  
So I'm hoping that tama naman yung ano.

**[02:07:35.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002275)  
I agree with the chair that we really have to stick to one.

**[02:07:39.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002276)  
So para in that way, we have yung recall, no?

**[02:07:45.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002277)  
That's difficult to...

**[02:07:47.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002278)  
To attain.

**[02:07:48.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002279)  
Si Mr. Chair, ang ano ko lang natin, no?

**[02:07:50.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002280)  
With the short presentation, although admittedly, we're only the fourth in terms of spending,

**[02:07:58.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002281)  
in terms of tourism promotions internationally.

**[02:08:03.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002282)  
Pero sa tourism arrivals, doon tayo naka, doon yun ang nakakalungkot, no?

**[02:08:08.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002283)  
We are one of the last, no?

**[02:08:11.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002284)  
Sa ASEAN countries.

**[02:08:12.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002285)  
Well, of course, Vietnam, kakasabi nyo lang, because of the private efforts, kakaiba, no?

**[02:08:17.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002286)  
Everyone's going to Vietnam now.

**[02:08:20.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002287)  
5.95 million visitors in 2025 compared to 14.35 million ng Indonesia pa ito.

**[02:08:30.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002288)  
And 17 million in Vietnam and Thailand, malaki rin.

**[02:08:34.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002289)  
So, what, ano?

**[02:08:38.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002290)  
In our present monitoring by DOT ng tourist arrivals, did we improve this year?

**[02:08:45.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002291)  
Ano po ang present, ano, natin?

**[02:08:47.300] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002292)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:08:48.820] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002293)  
As of August 2026, we have recorded 4.11 million foreign visitors na po.

**[02:08:55.060] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002294)  
It's up 3.7% from last year.

**[02:08:58.700] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002295)  
Our target po sana is 6.4 at the low end, 6.8 at the high end po.

**[02:09:04.920] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002296)  
So, while we are growing, we recognize po that we need to accelerate that growth in the remaining months po.

**[02:09:11.000] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002297)  
So, beyond spending for promotion, we would like to address the entire change.

**[02:09:17.300] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002298)  
First, air connectivity, kasi 99% of all travels to our country is by air.

**[02:09:24.420] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002299)  
So, from January to May 19 po, we already recorded 7.78 million international seats.

**[02:09:31.920] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002300)  
It's 8.31% year-on-year increase.

**[02:09:35.180] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002301)  
So, we are continuing to work with the airlines, the air operators, DOTR, to add routes and frequencies, particularly po sa secondary gateways.

**[02:09:45.300] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002302)  
For example po, China is good.

**[02:09:47.220] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002303)  
For example, kasi we have added direct routes from Hangzhou, Changsha, and Chongqing.

**[02:09:53.420] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002304)  
And ito po, from January to August, may kita niyo, tumaas po ang arrivals from China to 69.15%, about 310,000.

**[02:10:03.640] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002305)  
Tapos po, ang next strategy namin is we are becoming more market-specific.

**[02:10:08.360] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002306)  
Our largest markets from January to August was United States with 800,000 plus arrivals.

**[02:10:15.760] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002307)  
South Korea, second.

**[02:10:17.220] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002308)  
With 700,000 plus arrivals.

**[02:10:19.540] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002309)  
And Japan with 350,000 and China.

**[02:10:22.540] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002310)  
Australia po is 234,000.

**[02:10:25.140] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002311)  
So, these markets do not behave the same way.

**[02:10:28.660] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002312)  
So, our airline partnerships and tourism products will be tailored accordingly.

**[02:10:33.920] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002313)  
Thirdly po, we are making the Philippines easier to visit.

**[02:10:36.680] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002314)  
So, we are working po with DFA and other agencies para po matanggal yung friction, like visa entry facilitation.

**[02:10:43.960] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002315)  
Tapos, fourth, we are focusing po on value.

**[02:10:47.220] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002316)  
So, we want our visitors po to stay longer and spend more, travel beyond the major gateways.

**[02:10:54.760] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002317)  
And finally po, we are using tourism to attract investment.

**[02:10:58.440] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002318)  
TESA has already registered 14.5 billion in tourism investments across 34 projects under CREATE since 2021,

**[02:11:08.460] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002319)  
with 10 billion coming in 2020 through August 2026 alone.

**[02:11:14.260] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002320)  
At the same time po, just lumabas lang kahapon sa news.

**[02:11:17.220] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002321)  
The hotel sector reported a pipeline of approximately 387 billion in investments for 45,884 new hotel rooms across 213 projects.

**[02:11:30.200] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002322)  
So, we will continue po to market the Philippines, but we will also make it easier to reach and make it more accessible to move around.

**[02:11:38.900] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002323)  
That's it, sir. Thank you.

**[02:11:41.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002324)  
Well, we know the challenges, no?

**[02:11:43.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002325)  
As you mentioned, we are an archipelago.

**[02:11:47.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002326)  
Land, lahat sila, by land, no?

**[02:11:49.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002327)  
Karamihan, kaya mas marami.

**[02:11:51.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002328)  
Challenges, challenge talaga because by air, most of our arrivals, and marami din tayong challenge na kailangan nating ayusin.

**[02:11:58.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002329)  
Well, of course, our airports, for one, has to be more friendlier.

**[02:12:03.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002330)  
And I think, na iya naman is trying to, the new na iya management is trying to fix that, no?

**[02:12:08.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002331)  
In fact, in the other day, nung nagkaroon ng DOJ sa immigration natin, we also asked them, no?

**[02:12:15.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002332)  
Kasi there are several...

**[02:12:17.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002333)  
It's that they're installed, pero ang nakakagamit lang, napansin nga ni Senator Tulpo yun eh.

**[02:12:22.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002334)  
Bakit pag-arrival, ang nakakagamit lang, Pilipino, foreigners have to go through the usual immigration officers, no?

**[02:12:33.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002335)  
Pag naman doon sa departure, ang foreigner lang ang pwede, ang Pilipino, hindi.

**[02:12:38.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002336)  
Kailangan din sila, ano.

**[02:12:39.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002337)  
But they already presented that it's, magkakaroon sila ng gradual facing.

**[02:12:45.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002338)  
So, dahan-dahan.

**[02:12:47.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002339)  
They will be allowing already the use of e-gates more, no?

**[02:12:51.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002340)  
Para ma-utilize.

**[02:12:53.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002341)  
So, yun, convenience din kasi yun eh.

**[02:12:55.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002342)  
Diba?

**[02:12:55.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002343)  
I think, the seat part, ano, the seat, ano, malaking, I'm sure, Secretary, this is a big, ano, no?

**[02:13:01.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002344)  
Big factor din po yung convenience, saka yung connectivity natin.

**[02:13:05.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002345)

**[02:13:08.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002346)

**[02:13:10.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002347)  
So, there's a lot of challenges, we understand.

**[02:13:13.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002348)  
But, what else?

**[02:13:14.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002349)  
What else aside from promotion?

**[02:13:16.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002350)  
What else?

**[02:13:16.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002351)  
What other programs are you, are you doing to help increase our tourism?

**[02:13:23.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002352)  
Ang isang, yung nag-usapan namin ni Senator Tulfo, meron na ba tayong assessment nung competitiveness assessment?

**[02:13:29.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002353)  
Why is it that, why is it that our, our, some, some things like hotel, accommodation, hotels, restaurants sometimes are more expensive

**[02:13:41.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002354)  
kung sa Thailand, saka sa mga Vietnam, are cheaper, no?

**[02:13:45.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002355)  
Yung mga hotels.

**[02:13:46.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002356)  
So, ano ba ang difference?

**[02:13:48.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002357)  
And, what are we doing about this?

**[02:13:52.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002358)  
And, saka siguro, alam nyo naman yung nag-viral, yung pampanong 1,600, no?

**[02:13:58.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002359)  
Sa Bohol.

**[02:13:58.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002360)  
Bohol is such a beautiful province.

**[02:14:01.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002361)  
It's one of my favorite provinces na, unfortunately, their tourism arrivals also were affected this time.

**[02:14:08.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002362)  
So, are we looking at this?

**[02:14:11.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002363)  
Yung prices of our accommodation, yung hotels, prices of the...

**[02:14:16.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002364)  
of food, no?

**[02:14:18.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002365)  
Yung nangyari dito, no?

**[02:14:19.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002366)  
Sana hindi na maulit-ulit yun, no?

**[02:14:21.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002367)  
I think even the provincial government has stepped in.

**[02:14:24.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002368)  
So, siguro, in a nutshell, I know lots of challenges.

**[02:14:27.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002369)  
Siguro, Secdita, what, what, what else are we doing so that we can improve our arrivals?

**[02:14:34.560] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002370)  
Okay, sir.

**[02:14:35.200] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002371)  
Again, sir, we would like to highlight those other products na alam naming may pool, katulad po ng gastronomy, tourism po.

**[02:14:44.800] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002372)  
Since we engaged Michelin.

**[02:14:46.920] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002373)  
Kasi po, dumaki po ang transaction, both volume and value among those that have been recognized by Michelin po.

**[02:14:54.320] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002374)  
And then, you're correct, sir, about the restaurants.

**[02:14:57.060] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002375)  
Kasi, we are more expensive.

**[02:15:00.000] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002376)  
than other maybe Asian countries pagdating sa cost of food and services.

**[02:15:07.920] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002377)  
Pero sa food poster, it's a, I think, logistical issue.

**[02:15:11.760] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002378)  
It's, you know, we have to have better cold chain facility,

**[02:15:16.040] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002379)  
better roads, et cetera, to transport the items and produce in real time.

**[02:15:21.780] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002380)  
So I think that's something that we can work on together

**[02:15:24.380] DOT Secretary Dita Angara-Mathay** (asr-002381)  
with Department of Transportation and Agriculture.

**[02:15:30.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002382)  
Just quickly, Mr. Chair, with your permission,

**[02:15:33.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002383)  
there's a proposal to scrap travel tax, to ease travel costs.

**[02:15:39.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002384)  
But I just want to ask TESA if there are concrete revenue alternatives

**[02:15:45.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002385)  
presented to ensure it doesn't keep asking Congress directly

**[02:15:51.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002386)  
kasi mawawala yung sa inyo yan, ano?

**[02:15:54.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002387)  
Directly, kung mawawala ang travel tax, it will affect TESA,

**[02:15:57.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002388)  
yung infrastructure din natin.

**[02:16:00.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002389)  
Will be affected.

**[02:16:02.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002390)  
Sige, si GM, Dr. Mark Labid.

**[02:16:07.760] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002391)  
Thank you po.

**[02:16:08.320] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002392)  
With the permission of our Secretary,

**[02:16:10.620] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002393)  
Honorable Chairman, pleasant afternoon.

**[02:16:13.900] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002394)  
Mr. Chair, yes, regarding the issue of travel tax,

**[02:16:16.760] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002395)  
definitely we will be deeply affected

**[02:16:18.740] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002396)  
because 94% to 95% of our operation is coming from travel tax.

**[02:16:24.280] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002397)  
As a matter of fact, for 2027, Mr. Chair,

**[02:16:27.940] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002398)  
we already, as early as March,

**[02:16:30.000] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002399)  
we already submitted our proposed budget to DBM

**[02:16:34.440] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002400)  
since there's already a pending abolition of travel tax.

**[02:16:38.280] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002401)  
But I think in June, Mr. Chair, we received a letter coming from DBM

**[02:16:44.120] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002402)  
stating that they gave us zero budget allocation in the NEP.

**[02:16:49.560] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002403)  
So with that, Mr. Chair, wala akong binigay na item sa amin

**[02:16:54.540] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002404)  
or zero budget ang binigay sa amin ng DBM.

**[02:16:56.680] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002405)  
So what we did, we immediately revert muna.

**[02:17:00.000] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002406)  
to our board and submitted our corporate operating budget

**[02:17:04.380] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002407)  
for 2027, Mr. Chair.

**[02:17:07.400] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002408)  
Because as I mentioned a while ago,

**[02:17:10.340] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002409)  
we are heavily relying on the collection of travel tax,

**[02:17:15.920] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002410)  
which is around 4.03 billion for the CHESA share.

**[02:17:20.820] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002411)  
And the half of that is going to CHED and NCCA, Mr. Chair.

**[02:17:25.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002412)  
And the majority goes to CHESA.

**[02:17:27.920] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002413)  
50% po goes to us, sir.

**[02:17:30.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002414)  
40% goes to...

**[02:17:31.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002415)  
So on a nutshell, Mr. Chair, gusto natin may alamin ni Senator Tulfo,

**[02:17:35.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002416)  
ano-ano ba yung mga projects that, of course, yung mga infrastructure projects,

**[02:17:39.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002417)  
but what will, ano pa yung mga main?

**[02:17:41.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002418)  
Of course, yung mga, ano yan, di ba, yung mga lounge, mga comfort rooms.

**[02:17:47.580] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002419)  
Sir, not only that, yung mga vital projects po namin ngayon,

**[02:17:51.520] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002420)  
like yung water problem ng El Nido, we're addressing it.

**[02:17:55.380] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002421)  
Coron, we're the one who's doing the STP also.

**[02:17:58.320] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002422)  
The one in Baguio.

**[02:18:00.000] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002423)  
May rin po ang gumagawa.

**[02:18:01.260] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002424)  
And then we're also the one who's rehabilitating the 10 domestic airports that we have, Mr. Chair,

**[02:18:07.740] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002425)  
that was submitted to us by CAAP.

**[02:18:10.440] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002426)  
So we're rehabilitating.

**[02:18:11.860] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002427)  
Not to mention the hotels, di ba?

**[02:18:13.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002428)  
That's how you are also operating.

**[02:18:16.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002429)  
Malapit tayong Banawe, no?

**[02:18:18.220] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002430)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:18:18.640] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002431)  
Banawe, including the rehabilitation of Club Intramuros Golf Course also, Mr. Chair.

**[02:18:24.860] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002432)  
Sambuanga Golf Course.

**[02:18:26.360] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002433)  
So ito po yung mga nasa pipeline namin.

**[02:18:29.040] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002434)  
Aside from the other projects,

**[02:18:30.000] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002435)  
like the Burham Park.

**[02:18:30.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002436)  
How many hotels sa GM?

**[02:18:32.480] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002437)  
We have at least four, Mr. Chair, na hotels.

**[02:18:36.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002438)  
Ano to? Yung Mount Dada?

**[02:18:37.360] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002439)  
Mount Dada.

**[02:18:38.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002440)  
Mount Dada, Manawe.

**[02:18:39.860] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002441)  
Then Balikasag, Mr. Chair.

**[02:18:41.780] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002442)  
And then the other one is Gardens of Malasag, Mr. Chair.

**[02:18:46.120] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002443)  
So yan po yung mga areas namin na inaayos as of now.

**[02:18:50.460] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002444)  
But aside from this one, sir, we're also supporting the LGUs from other projects,

**[02:18:54.800] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002445)  
like as I mentioned a while ago, the rehabilitation of Burham Park sa Burakay,

**[02:19:00.000] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002446)  
pa rin po kami sa Siargao, we're also part on the rehabilitation.

**[02:19:05.200] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002447)  
So yun lang po ang amin na different tourist facilities ang aming binibuilt,

**[02:19:10.180] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002448)  
which is more on vertical, including on the PCSSD, on the dive tourism, Mr. Chair.

**[02:19:15.140] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002449)  
We're also providing the hyperbaric chamber sa mga different dive destinations po natin.

**[02:19:20.860] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002450)  
So these were all being funded by the travel tax, Mr. Chair.

**[02:19:24.680] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002451)  
So what is your request, Dr. Lapid?

**[02:19:30.000] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002452)  
Ano hong request ninyo?

**[02:19:31.920] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002453)  
Na po pwede ituloy muna hanggang December yung pagkukulek ng travel tax?

**[02:19:37.420] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002454)  
Sir, we humbly request to your honor, Mr. Chair,

**[02:19:41.980] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002455)  
baka pwede po munang magkaroon tayo ng transition period for the travel tax.

**[02:19:47.360] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002456)  
We don't object with any denotion of the abolishment, Mr. Chair,

**[02:19:51.600] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002457)  
but as of now, with the current fiscal situation, Mr. Chair,

**[02:19:55.940] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002458)  
medyo may hirapan ho ang ahensya namin, not only us,

**[02:20:00.000] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002459)  
the tourism industry, when it comes to the infrastructure support,

**[02:20:04.060] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002460)  
e baka magkaroon po, sir, ng konting consideration to expand it for the meantime.

**[02:20:09.280] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002461)  
Since for 2027, sir, we don't have any budget that was given to us.

**[02:20:15.480] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002462)  
So how are the obligated projects na ginagawa ho namin?

**[02:20:19.000] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002463)  
How are we going to continue and sustain?

**[02:20:21.260] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002464)  
Plus the other in our pipeline, paano ho yan, Mr. Chair,

**[02:20:24.300] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002465)  
which we're also asking DBM about that question.

**[02:20:28.040] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002466)  
So in the meantime, sir, kung habang wala,

**[02:20:29.860] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002467)  
kung wala pong pera ang national government,

**[02:20:31.960] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002468)  
we humbly seek na requesting na baka pwede magkaroon muna po ng extension for the meantime,

**[02:20:37.400] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002469)  
habang wala pong pera na pang-replace dun sa travel tax, Mr. Chair.

**[02:20:43.700] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002470)  
That's the reason why I invited DBM.

**[02:20:47.400] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002471)  
Hindi na sumipot. Natakot siguro. Baka walang masagot.

**[02:20:50.660] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002472)  
Baka nahihina sa pantalon yung tao na yan.

**[02:20:53.660] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002473)  
But anyway, sige po, we're looking to this.

**[02:20:56.700] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002474)  
We understand where you're coming from.

**[02:20:59.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002475)  
Mahirap nga ho yun.

**[02:21:01.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002475-split-2)  
Tama ka. Tama, Mr. Chair.

**[02:21:03.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002476)  
Pag nasa pipeline na yung project, anong gagawin? Hindi na po pondohan.

**[02:21:07.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002477)  
So kailangan ho siguro.

**[02:21:09.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002478)  
Pero kailangan, we have to sit down with DBM.

**[02:21:12.860] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002479)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[02:21:14.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002480)  
Unfortunately, hindi ko makita eh.

**[02:21:16.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002481)  
Pag nakita ko ho yung bukas.

**[02:21:18.860] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002482)  
Salamat po.

**[02:21:19.860] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002483)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[02:21:20.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002484)  
Thank you po.

**[02:21:21.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002485)  
Comsec, who's next to present?

**[02:21:23.860] Committee Secretary** (asr-002486)  
Next to present their budget is the Duty Free Philippines Corporation.

**[02:21:27.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002487)  
Duty Free, how long is your presentation?

**[02:21:29.700] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002488)  
Six slides, sir. Maybe less.

**[02:21:32.700] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002489)  
Okay, can you do that quickly?

**[02:21:34.700] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002490)  
Because meron po nang malakanyang na family day po ngayon.

**[02:21:38.700] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002491)  
Sumasobra na tayo.

**[02:21:40.700] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002492)  
Hanggang alas-singko lang dapat. Go ahead, proceed po.

**[02:21:42.700] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002493)  
Thank you. Good afternoon, Mr. Chair and distinguished members of the committee.

**[02:21:47.700] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002494)  
Allow me to present the, for year 2027, proposed corporate operating budget of Duty Free Philippines Corporation.

**[02:21:55.700] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002495)  
Next slide, please.

**[02:21:57.700] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002496)  
Next slide, please.

**[02:22:07.700] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002497)  
DFPC is a government corporation mandated to operate duty and tax-free merchandising system to serve tourists and returning Filipino travelers, generate foreign exchange, and contribute revenues to the government.

**[02:22:19.700] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002498)  
Our for year 2027 program is anchored on fulfilling this mandate while strengthening DFPC's sustainability.

**[02:22:26.700] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002499)  
With its existing 18 stores strategically located in international gateways.

**[02:22:33.700] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002500)  
Next slide, please.

**[02:22:36.700] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002501)  
Yeah, next slide.

**[02:22:39.700] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002502)  
So, for year 2027, DFPC proposes total sources of funds of 3.134 billion, of which 2.808 billion is internally generated revenue.

**[02:22:50.020] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002503)  
And 326.326 million

**[02:22:53.400] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002504)  
is DBM capitalization

**[02:22:55.600] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002505)  
pursuant to RA 9593.

**[02:22:58.820] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002506)  
Our total program uses amount to approximately 2.996 billion primarily for MOOE, capital investments,

**[02:23:08.300] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002507)  
and personal services.

**[02:23:10.000] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002508)  
The capitalization will support strategic investments intended to strengthen DFPC revenue generating capacity.

**[02:23:18.700] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002509)  
Next slide, please.

**[02:23:20.700] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002510)  
Next slide, please.

**[02:23:22.700] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002511)  
Sales declined significantly during the pandemic, reaching a low of approximately $35 million in 2021.

**[02:23:32.240] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002512)  
If you've noticed, sales recovered to $103 million in 2023, but declined to $82 million in 2025.

**[02:23:42.560] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002513)  
As of August 2026, sales stand at approximately $45.7 million.

**[02:23:49.280] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002514)  
And the main contributors to the decrease in sales primarily change in retail landscape, customer buying behavior, competition, high operating costs and airport concession fees, and of course, currency exchange volatility.

**[02:24:07.260] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002515)  
These figures highlight the need to strengthen our retail operations and extend our revenue base.

**[02:24:14.240] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002516)

**[02:24:16.560] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002517)

**[02:31:26.740] DFPC COO Patrick Joel M. Cinco** (asr-002776)

**[02:32:12.700] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002796)  
Go ahead, po. Go ahead.

**[02:32:13.840] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002797)  
Actually, sir, if directly to the budget, because we don't really secure any funds from the national government.

**[02:32:20.220] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002798)  
You don't have subsidies.

**[02:32:21.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002799)  
Sige, po. Sige, po.

**[02:32:22.040] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002800)  
Try to make your, make short your presentation, please.

**[02:32:24.800] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002801)  
So, the Nayong Pilipino Foundation.

**[02:32:25.920] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002802)  
Thank you, sir.

**[02:32:27.140] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002806)  
The Nayong Pilipino Foundation is focused on establishing a cultural hub that inspires Filipino creativity, green spaces, and sustainable park operation, in addition to supporting the Philippine tourism industry through research, development, and training.

**[02:32:43.920] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002807)  
Pending the development of the Nayong Pilipino Cultural Park, which is currently under review, we are at the helm of dissemination of our programs on Macanayon, Filipinization,

**[02:32:55.380] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002808)  
Palengke Tourism,

**[02:32:56.880] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002814)  
and soon our festival management program.

**[02:33:00.480] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002815)  
We have trained 1,246 persons from 17 regions from various schools and LGUs.

**[02:33:08.880] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002816)  
We have 3,000 artifacts for our museum,

**[02:33:11.840] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002817)  
which we have displayed at the Clark Expo site in Pampanga for 14 years from 2007 to 2021

**[02:33:20.180] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002818)  
and at the Tarlac Provincial Museum for the past three years, 2023 to 2026.

**[02:33:27.200] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002819)  
We now have select artifacts on display at the Ayala Museum,

**[02:33:32.300] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002820)  
the Talikang Pilipino Gallery II, the National Museum in Iloilo, and the National Museum in Zamboanga.

**[02:33:39.200] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002821)  
Our current space, while awaiting our park development program,

**[02:33:44.380] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002822)  
is currently maintaining a bike trail of 3 kilometers,

**[02:33:48.620] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002823)  
an arboretum with 3,000 native trees developed with the Energy Development Corporation,

**[02:33:54.600] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002824)  
an area for disc golf,

**[02:33:56.160] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002825)  
and an area for camping.

**[02:33:58.380] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002826)  
This is a favorite place for nature and sports enthusiasts,

**[02:34:01.960] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002827)  
and for 2025 till today, we have had 6,500 visitors.

**[02:34:07.900] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002828)  
Our park development program is a priority of the current Board of Trustees.

**[02:34:12.860] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002829)  
We are updating the Comprehensive Master Plan, which was prepared by a previous board in 2021.

**[02:34:19.560] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002830)  
We are updating the NEDA-approved 2 billion budget,

**[02:34:23.180] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002831)  
as endorsed by Secretary Frasco,

**[02:34:25.460] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002832)  
approved in 2024,

**[02:34:27.460] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002833)  
and effective till 2028.

**[02:34:29.460] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002834)  
We are reviewing other possible financial options with PPP, etc.,

**[02:34:35.460] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002835)  
and we are finalizing procurement process to have a consultant prepare our feasibility study.

**[02:34:41.460] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002836)  
We are eagerly moving forward on this project to be able to provide the Filipino people a truly cultural park they will enjoy and be proud of.

**[02:34:51.540] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002837)  
The Nayong Pilipino Foundation

**[02:34:53.320] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002838)  
does not receive a budget

**[02:34:54.920] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002839)  
from the national government.

**[02:34:56.460] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002840)  
Our resources come from

**[02:34:57.760] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002841)  
our corporate investment fund

**[02:34:59.260] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002842)  
and revenue from our activities.

**[02:35:01.780] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002843)  
We ensure that we only spend

**[02:35:03.580] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002844)  
what we have on hand.

**[02:35:05.740] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002849)  
But we also pay our taxes and dividends, and as of today, dividends, 17 million, real property taxes up to 2026 is 539 million, and BIR income taxes, a total of 173 million.

**[02:35:23.600] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002850)  
Our annual budget allocation is for payment of taxes alone, particularly RPT, is about 30% of our budget.

**[02:35:32.320] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002851)  
Our budget for 2027 is 73 million, with personnel services, 20 million, MOOE is 50 million, and capital outlay of 1.378.

**[02:35:46.940] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002852)  
We are aware that we also need to earn revenue from other sources, so we are working on the following revenue-generating activities.

**[02:35:56.300] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002853)  
We have a partnership with the National Youth Commission for the integration of the Bacanayan Cores,

**[02:36:01.900] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002854)  
partnership with

**[02:36:02.300] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002855)  
various colleges and universities,

**[02:36:04.500] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002856)  
partnership with the Department of Tourism and DILG for LGU training,

**[02:36:09.040] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002857)  
site activation projects like disc golf, biking, camping,

**[02:36:13.140] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002858)  
partnership with the Disc Golf Association for an international tournament with 12 countries,

**[02:36:19.700] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002859)  
and partnership with the Boy Scouts of the Philippines for their camping site.

**[02:36:24.100] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002860)  
Our other performance indicators include ARTA, Customer Satisfaction Rating of 97.82,

**[02:36:30.680] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002861)  
we are ISO,

**[02:36:32.300] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002862)  
9,001 certified,

**[02:36:34.800] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002863)  
our GCG PES scorecard is 97% in 2025,

**[02:36:40.020] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002864)  
our budget utilization is currently at 53%,

**[02:36:43.200] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002865)  
as we will still be paying our RPT year-end for the 2027 season.

**[02:36:49.980] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002866)  
So, this is my presentation now, Secretary, Senator, yeah.

**[02:36:57.260] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002867)  
Thank you, ma'am.

**[02:37:00.340] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002868)  
Tanong ko lang po.

**[02:37:06.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002870)

**[02:37:12.780] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002872)

**[02:37:15.100] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002873)

**[02:37:16.420] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002874)

Okay, kasi hinahanap ng anak ko, hindi ko rin ho alam eh.

**[02:37:57.900] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002888)

**[02:38:00.140] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002889)  
Yes.

**[02:38:00.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002890)  
Tapos marami po mga students.

**[02:38:01.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002891)  
Studyante.

**[02:38:01.860] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002892)  
Yes.

**[02:38:02.540] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002893)  
Tama po.

**[02:38:03.260] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002894)  
So, ngayon ba, may mandato po kayo, di ba, under presidential decree to establish parks, recreation center, libraries, etc., etc.?

**[02:38:15.560] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002895)  
Research, yes.

**[02:38:16.380] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002896)

**[02:38:23.660] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002897)  
Actually, the budget we have is 73M, but if we are able to get the NEDA budget,

**[02:38:30.460] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002898)  
All right.

**[02:38:31.180] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002899)  
2 billion, that was based on the 2021 study that was made, but probably by 2026, it would be much higher.

**[02:38:39.840] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002900)

**[02:38:45.860] NPF Executive Director Leonila R. Fabul** (asr-002901)  
Thank you, po.

Thank you.

**[02:38:47.100] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002903)  
All right.

**[02:38:55.200] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002906)  
Dr. Lapid, how long is your presentation, sir?

**[02:38:57.940] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002907)  
Sir, if I may, if we can just submit the...

**[02:39:00.540] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002908)

**[02:39:01.880] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002909)  
Can you just go directly to the budget?

**[02:39:03.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002910)  
And I know you're having a headache right now because once we remove that travel tax, you really have a big headache.

**[02:39:11.920] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002911)  
So, please go ahead.

**[02:39:16.660] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002912)  
For 2027, under our corporate operating budget, Mr. Chair, we submitted, our board has approved the 7.731,676 billion.

**[02:39:30.700] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002913)  
Together with the CHED share and the NCCA share.

**[02:39:35.280] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002914)  
For the TIEZA share, Mr. Chair, our proposed budget is 4.038 billion, which is a breakdown to our travel tax collection, which is 3.6.

**[02:39:49.120] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002915)  
Our receipts from the entities of 202 million, fees and charges coming from our tourism enterprise zones, which is 4.9.

**[02:39:56.440] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002916)  
Concession fee from our Puraki Island Water Company, 54 million.

**[02:40:01.000] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002917)  
From our other business income, which is 3.74.

**[02:40:05.460] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002918)  
That's coming from Corregidor and Hyperbatic Chamber.

**[02:40:08.880] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002919)  
Our other sources of income is 44.71.

**[02:40:12.840] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002920)  
That's coming from our rent, from our facilities and properties.

**[02:40:16.500] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002921)  
And our regulatory office, which is 35.48 million.

**[02:40:22.480] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002922)  
And our personal services is 738 million.

**[02:40:27.560] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002923)  
Our MOE is 1.077 billion.

**[02:40:30.540] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002924)  
Our equipment outlays 53.43 million.

**[02:40:33.820] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002925)  
And our improv project for 2027 will be 2.17 billion pesos, Mr. Chair.

**[02:40:42.360] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002926)  
So that would be for our budget for 2027, Mr. Chair.

**[02:40:46.440] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002927)  
One question lang po.

**[02:40:48.680] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002928)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:40:50.400] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002929)  
According to the Commission on Audit Findings, sir, Dr. Lapid,

**[02:40:55.260] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002930)  
Your office was not able to effectively implement daw a significant number,

**[02:41:00.700] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002931)  
of locally funded projects, with a contract cost of 3.3 billion.

**[02:41:04.800] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002932)

**[02:41:09.260] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002934)

**[02:41:17.260] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002936)

**[02:41:20.380] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002937)

**[02:41:26.920] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002938)  
para i- gawin po, pondohan yung Bayanihan [unclear] One.

**[02:41:42.080] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002947)

**[02:41:49.860] TIEZA COO Mark T. Lapid** (asr-002950)

Thank you, sir.

**[02:41:54.600] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002953)

**[02:41:55.340] Committee Secretary** (asr-002954)  
Next to present their budget is the Philippine Retirement Authority.

**[02:42:00.540] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002955)  
Good afternoon, Mr. Chair.

**[02:42:05.420] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002956)  
I'm not going to last very long because we are not asking for money from the government.

**[02:42:11.120] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002957)  
Instead, we're bringing money to the government.

**[02:42:13.680] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002958)  
That's good to hear.

**[02:42:16.580] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002959)  
Just give me five minutes so I can bring up what we have done so far for the government.

**[02:42:23.240] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002960)  
The mandate of PRA, Philippine Retirement Authority,

**[02:42:26.460] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002961)  
is really aside from making the Philippines haven,

**[02:42:30.540] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002962)  
and for foreign retirees,

**[02:42:32.540] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002963)  
is to increase the foreign currency reserve of our country.

**[02:42:37.840] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002964)
