**[02:42:41.540] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002965)  
we have in our banks a total of 654 million U.S. dollars.

**[02:42:49.680] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002966)  
And by the end of this year, we expect it to go beyond the 700 million mark.

**[02:42:56.220] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002967)  
And I think that alone is proof that we have reached,

**[02:43:00.680] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002968)  
our mandate.

**[02:43:01.720] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002969)  
The other mandate, as I mentioned earlier,

**[02:43:03.720] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002970)  
was to make the Philippines a haven for foreign retirees.

**[02:43:08.680] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002971)  
We just received two back-to-back awards,

**[02:43:12.320] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002972)  
international awards.

**[02:43:13.780] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002973)  
First, last year, TripZilla, which is a very popular booking platform,

**[02:43:20.200] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002974)  
declared that the Philippines is the best retirement destination in Asia.

**[02:43:26.600] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002975)  
Early this year, a London-based agency called

**[02:43:30.540] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002976)  
Expat Repatriation Index announced that the Philippines

**[02:43:36.080] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002977)  
is the number one retirement destination in the world.

**[02:43:40.340] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002978)  
Between the two, I have better respect for the second,

**[02:43:44.380] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002979)  
because the conclusion of coming up with the number one retirement destination for the Philippines

**[02:43:51.700] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002980)  
was based on a rating system.

**[02:43:54.200] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002981)  
They had five categories.

**[02:43:56.200] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002982)  
First category was accessibility of a retirement visa.

**[02:44:00.540] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002983)  
And they assigned 20 points for each category.

**[02:44:03.840] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002984)  
Twenty points for the accessibility of a retirement visa.

**[02:44:08.900] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002985)  
Twenty points for natural beauty of the country.

**[02:44:13.440] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002986)  
Third would be the cost of living.

**[02:44:15.380] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002987)  
Four would be hospitality index.

**[02:44:17.900] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002988)  
And the fifth would be healthcare.

**[02:44:20.640] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002989)  
We did very well in the first category,

**[02:44:24.480] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002990)  
which says so much about what the PRA has been doing.

**[02:44:29.680] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002991)  
we got a total, a perfect score of 20 points

**[02:44:33.780] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002992)  
for the first category, visa accessibility.

**[02:44:37.540] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002993)  
We didn't do too well in the healthcare category.

**[02:44:40.900] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002994)  
Thailand did better than us.

**[02:44:43.020] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002995)  
But when they totaled the whole thing,

**[02:44:45.580] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002996)  
we still ended up one point ahead of Thailand.

**[02:44:48.780] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002997)  
That's why we became the number one destination for the world.

**[02:44:53.620] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002998)  
Now, why is that important?

**[02:44:54.960] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-002999)  
The total, the global retirement in...

**[02:45:00.000] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003000)  
industry is estimated

**[02:45:01.920] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003001)  
to be valued at

**[02:45:03.220] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003002)  
70 trillion US dollars.

**[02:45:06.640] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003003)  
Now, if we are the number

**[02:45:07.900] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003004)  
one choice to be the retirement

**[02:45:09.940] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003005)  
destination, then we can get

**[02:45:11.840] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003006)  
a good chunk of that amount

**[02:45:13.540] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003007)  
as long as we continue to

**[02:45:15.840] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003008)  
do what we're doing now, promoting

**[02:45:17.780] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003009)  
to different parts of the world.

**[02:45:19.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003010)  
Sir, let me just ask you this question.

**[02:45:21.800] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003011)  
What are the basis for these people

**[02:45:24.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003012)  
to decide

**[02:45:26.060] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003013)

**[02:45:28.040] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003014)

**[02:45:28.680] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003015)

**[02:45:35.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003016)

**[02:45:37.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003017)  
Is it tourism infrastructure?

**[02:45:40.100] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003018)  
Is it safety?

**[02:45:42.640] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003019)  
Feeling safe na

**[02:45:44.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003020)  
They will not be harmed

**[02:45:46.140] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003021)  
while they're here in the country

**[02:45:47.460] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003022)  
as they grow old?

**[02:45:48.980] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003023)  
What is it?

**[02:45:49.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003024)

**[02:45:53.340] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003025)  
No, that's last.

**[02:45:55.660] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003026)  
The beautiful women

**[02:45:56.980] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003027)  
is being considered.

**[02:45:57.780] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003028)  
I mean, it might sound like a joke,

**[02:46:00.040] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003029)  
but what I'm saying, I'm proud of Filipinas.

**[02:46:02.400] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003030)  
Yes.

**[02:46:02.820] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003031)  
Well, we have beautiful ladies.

**[02:46:05.760] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003032)  
That's why we always win

**[02:46:07.420] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003033)  
in the international beauty pageants.

**[02:46:09.380] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003034)  
Yes.

**[02:46:09.800] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003035)  
Because we have beautiful women.

**[02:46:11.940] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003036)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:46:12.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003037)  
Kaya nga, that's my question to you.

**[02:46:14.440] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003038)  
Ano, which part of it?

**[02:46:15.960] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003039)  
Is it safety?

**[02:46:18.040] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003040)  
Is it mura, the economy

**[02:46:20.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003041)  
that their money's worth?

**[02:46:22.040] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003042)  
Is it tourism?

**[02:46:24.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003043)  
I mean, good surroundings?

**[02:46:26.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003044)  
What is it?

**[02:46:27.780] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003045)  
The number one criterion

**[02:46:29.180] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003046)  
that they consider

**[02:46:30.040] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003047)  
is the cost of living.

**[02:46:32.140] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003048)  
That's number one, cost of living.

**[02:46:33.560] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003049)  
The second factor is hospitality.

**[02:46:36.060] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003050)  
Our people's welcoming attitude.

**[02:46:38.220] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003051)  
They like that.

**[02:46:39.320] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003052)  
And the fact that we speak English

**[02:46:41.440] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003053)  
is also a very important factor for them.

**[02:46:45.300] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003054)  
And all the others follow.

**[02:46:47.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003055)  
Okay.

**[02:46:48.420] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003056)  
My last question for you, sir.

**[02:46:51.220] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003057)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:46:51.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003058)  
Is that in light of the Alice Guo case,

**[02:46:57.780] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003059)  
broader concerns it raised regarding the entry

**[02:47:00.020] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003060)  
and presence of foreign nationals in the Philippines,

**[02:47:03.380] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003061)  
how do you monitor that these people are really legit?

**[02:47:07.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003062)  
They're really retirees

**[02:47:08.680] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003063)  
or probably they're just spies from other countries, sir.

**[02:47:13.380] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003064)  
And how would you monitor that?

**[02:47:16.020] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003065)  
Na hindi na tayo napapasok dito ng mga ikang spies

**[02:47:23.380] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003066)  
in this country?

**[02:47:24.500] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003067)  
Kasi binabaan pa natin yung retirement age.

**[02:47:27.780] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003068)  
Yeah.

**[02:47:28.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003069)  
Go ahead.

**[02:47:29.780] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003070)  
First of all, before the monitoring system comes in,

**[02:47:33.080] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003071)  
the vetting process,

**[02:47:35.780] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003072)  
we improved the security measures

**[02:47:38.280] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003073)  
that we impose on these applicants,

**[02:47:40.980] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003074)  
especially for the PROC Chinese.

**[02:47:44.180] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003075)  
And we improve them by adding additional security measure.

**[02:47:50.080] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003076)  
For example,

**[02:47:50.880] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003077)  
we hired our own military intelligence officer

**[02:47:56.380] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003078)  
as our security.

**[02:47:57.780] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003079)  
Officer.

**[02:47:58.780] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003080)  
And he interviews all applicants face to face.

**[02:48:04.280] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003081)  
If cannot, it can be via Zoom.

**[02:48:06.280] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003082)  
But there's always a visual contact.

**[02:48:09.280] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003083)  
And this, the other thing that we did is many years ago

**[02:48:15.280] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003084)  
when these applicants, especially the Chinese,

**[02:48:19.280] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003085)  
they brought in their own interpreters.

**[02:48:21.720] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003086)  
So what we did, we hired our own interpreter

**[02:48:24.520] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003087)  
so that

**[02:48:26.060] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003088)  
you can get what

**[02:48:27.480] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003089)  
the red flags are during the conversation.

**[02:48:29.820] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003090)  
So all these additional features, sir,

**[02:48:32.360] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003091)  
we presented it before we could implement everything.

**[02:48:39.080] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003092)  
We presented the whole list of additional security measures

**[02:48:43.080] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003093)  
to former Secretary A?o of the NSC.

**[02:48:46.080] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003094)  
And he went over it and he approved of it.

**[02:48:49.080] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003095)  
And what I told him is,

**[02:48:51.080] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003096)  
if you could please write down your approval.

**[02:48:53.580] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003097)  
So he issued us a letter saying that it's enough.

**[02:48:57.580] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003098)  
The monitoring aspect,

**[02:48:59.080] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003099)  
the moment we come, we admit these additional applicants

**[02:49:04.580] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003100)  
and we approve their retirement visas,

**[02:49:07.580] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003101)  
we send their information to NICA every week.

**[02:49:11.080] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003102)  
A list of new retirees that have availed of our retirement visa

**[02:49:16.080] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003103)  
is given to NICA for monitoring.

**[02:49:19.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003104)  
How do they, how do you check their backgrounds?

**[02:49:21.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003105)  
I mean, you can only check their backgrounds

**[02:49:24.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003106)  
when they're already here or

**[02:49:27.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003107)  
or kahit na nandoon pa sila in another country,

**[02:49:29.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003108)  
in their own country.

**[02:49:30.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003109)  
Is there a way to check it?

**[02:49:32.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003110)  
Baka mamaya ito ay pedophile, itong retiree na ito.

**[02:49:35.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003111)  
Itong retiree na ito ay may kasong murder.

**[02:49:39.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003112)  
And then just want to retire or running away from their own laws.

**[02:49:44.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003113)  
Baka wanted po yun.

**[02:49:46.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003114)  
So how do you do that?

**[02:49:48.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003115)  
How do you check?

**[02:49:49.080] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003116)  
What we do, sir, is we have access to the Interpol database.

**[02:49:55.080] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003117)  
So we can check every applicant.

**[02:49:57.580] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003118)  
Aside from that, we require each applicant

**[02:50:00.580] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003119)  
to present to us a police clearance from their country of origin

**[02:50:04.580] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003120)  
and it must be apostilled

**[02:50:06.580] PRA General Manager and CEO Roberto Z. Zozobrado** (asr-003121)  
to be sure that it's factual and correct.

**[02:50:09.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003122)  
Alright, thank you, sir. Thank you, Mr. Sosobrado.

**[02:50:13.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003123)  
Let me go to Yusec Azurin.

**[02:50:15.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003124)  
Sir, anong ginagawa ninyo or what have you done?

**[02:50:18.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003125)  
May adjustments? Itong pong mga Israeli sa Siargao,

**[02:50:22.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003126)  
natapos na po ba yan?

**[02:50:24.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003127)  
Yung mga, yung holdapan po sa

**[02:50:27.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003128)  
El Nido.

**[02:50:29.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003129)  
Dabagamat hindi naman foreigners ang in-hold up,

**[02:50:31.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003130)  
kundi mga local community stores po doon.

**[02:50:34.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003131)  
How do you step up yung security of our tourists?

**[02:50:37.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003132)  
This is one of the reasons why medyo nagdadalawang isip sila.

**[02:50:41.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003133)  
I'd rather go to Vietnam kasi medyo tahimik.

**[02:50:45.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003134)  
I'd rather go, sasabihin, I'd rather go to Thailand, medyo safe doon.

**[02:50:49.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003135)  
So how, how, what are you doing right now?

**[02:50:53.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003136)  
Or what can be done?

**[02:50:54.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003137)  
I mean, may nakikita akong tourist police,

**[02:50:56.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003138)  
pero sa umaga lang.

**[02:50:58.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003139)  
Sa gabi, when I go around sa mga island tourist destinations,

**[02:51:04.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003140)  
parang nawawala na yung police natin sa umaga lang.

**[02:51:07.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003141)  
Or dito sa Metro Manila, nakikita ko sa gabi, palakad-lakad sila.

**[02:51:11.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003142)  
So anong adjustments ang ginagawa po ng DOT pertaining to this, to the safety?

**[02:51:17.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003143)  
Kasi yung pang-una magiging concern eh.

**[02:51:19.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003144)  
Will I, shall I bring my family to the Philippines?

**[02:51:22.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003145)  
My wife and my two kids?

**[02:51:24.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003146)  
Will it be safe if we go around the streets at night?

**[02:51:28.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003147)  
In Boracay, in El Nido, in wherever?

**[02:51:33.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003148)  
Will it be safe?

**[02:51:35.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-003149)  
Ano ang programa nyo rito, Yusek Azurin?

**[02:51:40.580] DOT Undersecretary Rodolfo Azurin Jr.** (asr-003150)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair, sir.

**[02:51:42.580] DOT Undersecretary Rodolfo Azurin Jr.** (asr-003151)  
Good evening po.

**[02:51:43.580] DOT Undersecretary Rodolfo Azurin Jr.** (asr-003152)  
I think, sir, one of the programs that we are pushing with the Department of Tourism right now is

**[02:51:51.580] DOT Undersecretary Rodolfo Azurin Jr.** (asr-003153)  
to coordinate with the security sector.

**[02:51:54.580] DOT Undersecretary Rodolfo Azurin Jr.** (asr-003154)  
The Philippine National Police, the Armed Forces of the Philippines and the Philippine Coast Guard

**[02:52:00.580] DOT Undersecretary Rodolfo Azurin Jr.** (asr-003155)  
because they are a factor, sir, in keeping our country safe and secure.

**[02:52:07.580] DOT Undersecretary Rodolfo Azurin Jr.** (asr-003156)  
And as a matter of fact, with respect to the problem in Siargao,

**[02:52:12.580] DOT Undersecretary Rodolfo Azurin Jr.** (asr-003157)  
more or less, sir, mayroon tayong 500 na Israeli doon.

**[02:52:17.580] DOT Undersecretary Rodolfo Azurin Jr.** (asr-003158)  
And sometimes, yung report kasi, sir, masyadong na-hype.

**[02:52:22.580] DOT Undersecretary Rodolfo Azurin Jr.** (asr-003159)  
But for this year alone,

**[02:52:24.580] DOT Undersecretary Rodolfo Azurin Jr.** (asr-003160)  
there are only about eight na cases against the Israelis

**[02:52:31.580] DOT Undersecretary Rodolfo Azurin Jr.** (asr-003161)  
na they committed against other foreign visitors and sa mga locals po.

**[02:52:38.580] DOT Undersecretary Rodolfo Azurin Jr.** (asr-003162)  
And these cases are already solved, sir.

**[02:52:42.580] DOT Undersecretary Rodolfo Azurin Jr.** (asr-003163)
